<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Now it came to pass in the thirtieth year, in the fourth &#91;month&#93;, in the fifth &#91;day&#93; of the month, as I &#91;was&#93; among the captives by the river of, &#91;that&#93; the heavens were opened, and I saw visions of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> In the fifth &#91;day&#93; of the month, which &#91;was&#93; the fifth year of king Y’hoyakhin’s &#91;Yehovah establishes&#93;’s captivity,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came expressly unto Yechezk’el &#91;God strengthens&#93; the priest, the son of Buzi &#91;my contempt&#93;, in the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93; by the river K’var &#91;far-off&#93;; and the hand of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was there upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> And I looked, and, behold, a whirlwind came out of the north, a great cloud, and a fire infolding itself, and a brightness &#91;was&#93; about it, and out of the midst thereof as the colour of amber, out of the midst of the fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Also out of the midst thereof &#91;came&#93; the likeness of four living creatures. And this &#91;was&#93; their appearance; they had the likeness of a man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And everyone had four faces, and everyone had four wings.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And their feet &#91;were&#93; straight feet; and the sole of their feet &#91;was&#93; like the sole of a calf’s foot: and they sparkled like the colour of burnished brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And &#91;they had&#93; the hands of a man under their wings on their four sides; and they four had their faces and their wings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Their wings &#91;were&#93; joined one to another; they turned not when they went; they went everyone straight forward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> As for the likeness of their faces, they four had the face of a man, and the face of a lion, on the right side: and they four had the face of an ox on the left side; they four also had the face of an eagle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Thus &#91;were&#93; their faces: and their wings &#91;were&#93; stretched upward; two &#91;wings&#93; of everyone &#91;were&#93; joined one to another, and two covered their bodies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And they went everyone straight forward: to which the spirit was to go, they went; &#91;and&#93; they turned not when they went.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> As for the likeness of the living creatures, their appearance &#91;was&#93; like burning coals of fire, &#91;and&#93; like the appearance of lamps: it went up and down among the living creatures; and the fire was bright, and out of the fire went forth lightning.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And the living creatures ran and returned as the appearance of a flash of lightning.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Now as I beheld the living creatures, behold one wheel upon the earth by the living creatures, with his four faces.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> The appearance of the wheels and their work &#91;was&#93; like unto the colour of a beryl: and they four had one likeness: and their appearance and their work &#91;was&#93; as it were a wheel in the middle of a wheel.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> When they went, they went upon their four sides: &#91;and&#93; they turned not when they went.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And when the living creatures went, the wheels went by them: and when the living creatures were lifted up from the earth, the wheels were lifted up.</span>

</div>

</div>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Son of man, I have made youi a watchman unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: therefore hear the word at my mouth, and give them warning from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> When I say unto the wicked, youi shall surely die; and youi give him not warning, nor speak to warn the wicked from his wicked way, to save his life; the same wicked &#91;man&#93; shall die in his iniquity; but his blood will I require at youri hand.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> But when I speak with youi, I will open youri mouth, and youi shall say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); He that hears, let him hear; and he that forbears, let him refrain (restrain; refrein from acting): for they &#91;are&#93; a rebellious house.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Youi also, Son of man, take youi a tile, and lay it before youi, and portray upon it the city, &#91;even&#93; Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Lie youi also upon youri left side, and lay the iniquity of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; upon it: &#91;according&#93; to the number of the days that youi shall lie upon it youi shall bear their iniquity.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Therefore youi shall set youri face toward the siege of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and youri arm &#91;shall be&#93; uncovered, and youi shall prophesy against it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And, behold, I will lay bands upon youi, and youi shall not turn youi from one side to another, till youi have ended the days of youri siege.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Take youi also unto youi wheat, and barley, and beans, and lentiles, and millet, and fitches (a kind of grain used for seasoning), and put them in one vessel, and make youi bread thereof, &#91;according&#93; to the number of the days that youi shall lie upon youi side, three hundred and ninety days shall youi eat thereof.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Youi shall drink also water by measure, the sixth part of an hin &#91;a quart&#93;: from time to time shall youi drink.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And youi shall eat it &#91;as&#93; barley cakes, and youi shall bake it with dung that comes out of man, in their sight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said, Even thus shall the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; eat their defiled bread among the Gentiles, to which I will drive them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Then said I, Ah Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER)! behold, my soul has not been polluted: for from my youth up even till now have I not eaten of that which dies of itself, or is torn in pieces; neither came there abominable flesh into my mouth.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Moreover he said unto me, Son of man, behold, I will break the staff of bread in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: and they shall eat bread by weight, and with care; and they shall drink water by measure, and with astonishment:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> That they may lack bread and water, and be astonished one with another, and consume away for their iniquity.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And youi, Son of man, take youi a sharp knife, take youi a barber’s razor, and cause &#91;it&#93; to pass upon youri head and upon youri beard: then take youi balances to weigh, and divide the &#91;hair&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Youi shall burn with fire a third part in the midst of the city, when the days of the siege are fulfilled: and youi shall take a third part, &#91;and&#93; strike about it with a knife: and a third part youi shall scatter in the wind; and I will draw out a sword after them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); This &#91;is&#93; Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;: I have set it in the midst of the nations and countries &#91;that are&#93; round about her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And she has changed my judgments into wickedness more than the nations, and my statutes more than the countries that &#91;are&#93; round about her: for they have refused my judgments and my statutes, they have not walked in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because you2f multiplied more than the nations that &#91;are&#93; round about you, &#91;and&#93; have not walked in my statutes, neither have kept my judgments, neither have done according to the judgments of the nations that &#91;are&#93; round about you;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I, even I, &#91;am&#93; against youi, and will execute judgments in the midst of youri in the sight of the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And I will do in youi that which I have not done, and whereunto I will not do any more the like, because of all youi abominations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Therefore the fathers shall eat the sons in the midst of youi, and the sons shall eat their fathers; and I will execute judgments in youi, and the whole remnant of youi will I scatter into all the winds.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> For what reason, &#91;as&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Surely, because youi have defiled my sanctuary with all youri detestable things, and with all youri abominations, therefore will I also diminish &#91;youi&#93;; neither shall my eye spare, neither will I have any pity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> A third part of youi shall die with the pestilence, and with famine shall they be consumed in the midst of youi: and a third part shall fall by the sword round about youi; and I will scatter a third part into all the winds, and I will draw out a sword after them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Thus shall my anger be accomplished, and I will cause my fury to rest upon them, and I will be comforted: and they shall know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93; in my zeal, when I have accomplished my fury in them.</span>

</div>

I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;.</span>

I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, set youri face toward the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and prophesy against them,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say, You2f mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, hear the word of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) to the mountains, and to the hills, to the rivers, and to the valleys; Behold, I, &#91;even&#93; I, will bring a sword upon you, and I will destroy your high places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> In all your dwelling places the cities shall be laid waste, and the high places shall be desolate; that your altars may be laid waste and made desolate, and your idols may be broken and cease, and your images may be cut down, and your works may be abolished.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Yet will I leave a remnant, that you2f may have &#91;some&#93; that shall escape the sword among the nations, when you2f shall be scattered through the countries.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And they that escape of you shall remember me among the nations to which they shall be carried captives, because I am broken with their whorish heart, which has departed from me, and with their eyes, which go a whoring after their idols: and they shall loathe themselves for the evils which they have committed in all their abominations.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Strike with youri hand, and stamp with youri foot, and say, Alas for all the evil abominations of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;! for they shall fall by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> He that is far off shall die of the pestilence; and he that is near shall fall by the sword; and he that remains and is besieged shall die by the famine: thus will I accomplish my fury upon them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Then shall you2f know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when their slain &#91;men&#93; shall be among their idols round about their altars, upon every high hill, in all the tops of the mountains, and under every green tree, and under every thick oak, the place where they did offer soothing aroma to all their idols.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Also, youi Son of man, thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) unto the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; An end, the end is come upon the four corners of the land.</span>

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> And my eye shall not spare youi, neither will I have pity: but I will recompense youri ways upon youi, and youri abominations shall be in the midst of youi: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); An evil, an only evil, behold, is come.</span>

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> An end is come, the end is come: it watch for youi; behold, it is come.</span>

<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> The morning is come unto youi, O youi that dwell in the land: the time is come, the day of trouble &#91;is&#93; near, and not the sounding again of the mountains.</span>

<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Now will I shortly pour out my fury upon youi, and accomplish my anger upon youi: and I will judge youi according to youri ways, and will recompense youi for all youri abominations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And my eye shall not spare, neither will I have pity: I will recompense youi according to youri ways and youri abominations &#91;that&#93; are in the midst of youi; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; Yehovah Makeh &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate Striking &#123;You&#125;&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Behold the day, behold, it is come: the morning is gone forth; the rod has blossomed, pride has budded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Violence is risen up into a rod of wickedness: none of them &#91;shall remain&#93;, nor of their multitude, nor of any of theirs: neither &#91;shall there be&#93; wailing for them.</span>

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> The time is come, the day draws near: let not the buyer rejoice, nor the seller mourn: for wrath &#91;is&#93; upon all the multitude thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> For the seller shall not return to that which is sold, although they were yet alive: for the vision &#91;is&#93; touching the whole multitude thereof, &#91;which&#93; shall not return; neither shall any strengthen himself in the iniquity of his life.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> The sword &#91;is&#93; without, and the pestilence and the famine within: he that &#91;is&#93; in the field shall die with the sword; and he that &#91;is&#93; in the city, famine and pestilence shall devour him.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> But they that escape of them shall escape, and shall be on the mountains like doves of the valleys, all of them mourning, everyone for his iniquity.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> All hands shall be feeble, and all knees shall be weak &#91;as&#93; water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> They shall also gird &#91;themselves&#93; with sackcloth, and horror shall cover them; and shame &#91;shall be&#93; upon all faces, and baldness upon all their heads.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> They shall cast their silver in the streets, and their gold shall be removed: their silver and their gold shall not be able to deliver them in the day of the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): they shall not satisfy their souls, neither fill their bowels (inward parts; affections): because it is the stumbling block of their iniquity.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> As for the beauty of his ornament, he set it in majesty: but they made the images of their abominations &#91;and&#93; of their detestable things in it: therefore have I set it far from them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And I will give it into the hands of the strangers for a prey, and to the wicked of the earth for a spoil (plunder; prey; booty); and they shall pollute it.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> My face will I turn also from them, and they shall pollute my secret &#91;place&#93;: for the robbers shall enter into it, and defile it.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Make a chain: for the land is full of bloody crimes, and the city is full of violence.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> For what reason I will bring the worst of the heathen, and they shall possess their houses: I will also make the pomp of the strong to cease; and their holy places shall be defiled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Destruction comes; and they shall seek peace, and &#91;there shall be&#93; none.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> Mischief shall come upon mischief, and rumour shall be upon rumour; then shall they seek a vision of the prophet; but the law shall perish from the priest, and counsel from the ancients.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> The king shall mourn, and the prince shall be clothed with desolation, and the hands of the people of the land shall be troubled: I will do unto them after their way, and according to their deserts will I judge them; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And it came to pass in the sixth year, in the sixth &#91;month&#93;, in the fifth &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;as&#93; I sat in my house, and the elders of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; sat before me, that the hand of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) fell there upon me.</span>
