</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Therefore say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Although I have cast them far off among the heathen, and although I have scattered them among the countries, yet will I be to them as a little sanctuary in the countries where they shall come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Therefore say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will even gather you from the people, and assemble you out of the countries where you2f have been scattered, and I will give you the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> And they shall come there, and they shall take away all the detestable things thereof and all the abominations thereof from there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And I will give them one heart, and I will put a new spirit within you; and I will take the stony heart out of their flesh, and will give them an heart of flesh:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> That they may walk in my statutes, and keep my ordinances, and do them: and they shall be my people, and I will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> But &#91;as for them&#93; whose heart walks after the heart of their detestable things and their abominations, I will recompense their way upon their own heads, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Then did the k’vurim (cherubim) lift up their wings, and the wheels beside them; and the glory of the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;was&#93; over them above.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> And the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) went up from the midst of the city, and stood upon the mountain which &#91;is&#93; on the east side of the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> Afterwards the spirit took me up, and brought me in a vision by the Spirit of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; into Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, to them of the captivity. So the vision that I had seen went up from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Then I spoke unto them of the captivity all the things that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had showed me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) also came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, youi dwell in the midst of a rebellious house, which have eyes to see, and see not; they have ears to hear, and hear not: for they &#91;are&#93; a rebellious house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Therefore, youi Son of man, prepare youri stuff for removing, and remove by day in their sight; and youi shall remove from youri place to another place in their sight: it may be they will consider, though they &#91;be&#93; a rebellious house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Then shall youi bring forth youri stuff by day in their sight, as stuff for removing: and youi shall go forth at even in their sight, as they that go forth into captivity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Dig youi through the wall in their sight, and carry out thereby.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> In their sight shall youi bear &#91;it&#93; upon &#91;youri&#93; shoulders, &#91;and&#93; carry &#91;it&#93; forth in the twilight: youi shall cover youri face, that youi see not the ground: for I have set youi &#91;for&#93; a sign unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And I did so as I was commanded: I brought forth my stuff by day, as stuff for captivity, and in the even I excavated through the wall with my hand; I brought &#91;it&#93; forth in the twilight, &#91;and&#93; I bore &#91;it&#93; upon &#91;my&#93; shoulder in their sight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And in the morning came the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Son of man, has not the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, the rebellious house, said unto youi, What do youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Say youi unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); This burden &#91;concern&#93; the prince in Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and all the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that &#91;are&#93; among them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Say, I &#91;am&#93; your sign: like as I have done, so shall it be done unto them: they shall remove &#91;and&#93; go into captivity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And the prince that &#91;is&#93; among them shall bear upon &#91;his&#93; shoulder in the twilight, and shall go forth: they shall dig through the wall to (to do entirely, do wholly) carry out thereby: he shall cover his face, that he see not the ground with &#91;his&#93; eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> My net also will I spread upon him, and he shall be taken in my snare: and I will bring him to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; &#91;to&#93; the land of the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;; yet shall he not see it, though he shall die there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And I will scatter toward every wind all that &#91;are&#93; about him to help him, and all his bands; and I will draw out the sword after them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> And they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I shall scatter them among the nations, and disperse them in the countries.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> But I will leave a few men of them from the sword, from the famine, and from the pestilence; that they may declare all their abominations among the heathen to which they come; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came to me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> Son of man, eat youri bread with quaking, and drink youri water with trembling and with carefulness;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And say unto the people of the land, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) of the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, &#91;and&#93; of the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; They shall eat their bread with carefulness, and drink their water with astonishment, that her land may be desolate from all that is in it, because of the violence of all them that dwell in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> And the cities that are inhabited shall be laid waste, and the land shall be desolate; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Son of man, what &#91;is&#93; that proverb &#91;that&#93; you2f have in the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, The days are prolonged, and every vision fails?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Tell them therefore, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will make this proverb to cease, and they shall no more use it as a proverb in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; but say unto them, The days are at hand, and the effect of every vision.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> For there shall be no more any vain vision nor flattering divination within the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> For I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): I will speak, and the word that I shall speak shall come to pass; it shall be no more prolonged: for in your days, O rebellious house, will I say the word, and will perform it, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> Again the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came to me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Son of man, behold, &#91;they of&#93; the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; say, The vision that he sees &#91;is&#93; for many days &#91;to come&#93;, and he prophesies of the times &#91;that are&#93; far off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> Therefore say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); There shall none of my words be prolonged any more, but the word which I have spoken shall be done, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, prophesy against the prophets of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that prophesy, and say youi unto them that prophesy out of their own hearts, Hear you2f the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Woe unto the foolish prophets, that follow their own spirit, and have seen nothing!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, youri prophets are like the foxes in the deserts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> You2f have not gone up into the gaps, neither made up the hedge for the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to stand in the battle in the day of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> They have seen vanity and lying divination, saying, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) says: and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has not sent them: and they have made &#91;others&#93; to hope that they would confirm the word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Have you2f not seen a vain vision, and have you2f not spoken a lying divination, whereas you2f say, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) says &#91;it&#93;; albeit (although it be that) I have not spoken?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because you2f have spoken vanity, and seen lies, therefore, behold, I &#91;am&#93; against you, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And my hand shall be upon the prophets that see vanity, and that divine lies: they shall not be in the assembly of my people, neither shall they be written in the writing of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, neither shall they enter into the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Because, even because they have seduced my people, saying, Peace; and &#91;there was&#93; no peace; and one built up a wall, and, lo, others daubed it with untempered &#91;mortar&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Say unto them which daub (to cover or plaster) &#91;it&#93; with untempered &#91;mortar&#93;, that it shall fall: there shall be an overflowing shower; and you2f, O great hailstones, shall fall; and a stormy wind shall rend (tear apart; forcefully rip apart) &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Lo, when the wall is fallen, shall it not be said unto you, Where &#91;is&#93; the daubing which you2f have daubed &#91;it&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will even rend (tear apart; forcefully rip apart) &#91;it&#93; with a stormy wind in my fury; and there shall be an overflowing shower in my anger, and great hailstones in &#91;my&#93; fury to consume &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> So will I break down the wall that you2f have daubed with untempered &#91;mortar&#93;, and bring it down to the ground, so that the foundation thereof shall be discovered, and it shall fall, and you2f shall be consumed in the midst thereof: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Thus will I accomplish my wrath upon the wall, and upon them that have daubed it with untempered &#91;mortar&#93;, and will say unto you, The wall &#91;is&#93; no &#91;more&#93;, neither they that daubed it;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> &#91;To wit&#93;, the prophets of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; which prophesy concerning Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and which see visions of peace for her, and &#91;there is&#93; no peace, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Likewise, youi Son of man, set youri face against the daughters of youri people, which prophesy out of their own heart; and prophesy youi against them,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> And say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Woe to the &#91;women&#93; that sew pillows to all armholes, and make kerchiefs upon the head of every stature to hunt souls! Will you2f hunt the souls of my people, and will you2f save the souls alive &#91;that come&#93; unto you?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And will you2f pollute me among my people for handfuls of barley and for pieces of bread, to slay the souls that should not die, and to save the souls alive that should not live, by your lying to my people that hear &#91;your&#93; lies?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> For what reason thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against your pillows, which you2f there hunt the souls to make &#91;them&#93; fly, and I will tear them from your arms, and will let the souls go, &#91;even&#93; the souls that you2f hunt to make &#91;them&#93; fly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> Your kerchiefs also will I tear, and deliver my people out of your hand, and they shall be no more in your hand to be hunted; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Because with lies you2f have made the heart of the righteous sad, whom I have not made sad; and strengthened the hands of the wicked, that he should not return from his wicked way, by promising him life:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Therefore you2f shall see no more vanity, nor divine divinations: for I will deliver my people out of your hand: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Then came certain of the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; unto me, and sat before me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Son of man, these men have set up their idols in their heart, and put the stumbling block of their iniquity before their face: should I be enquired of at all by them?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Therefore speak unto them, and say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Every man of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that sets up his idols in his heart, and puts the stumblingblock of his iniquity before his face, and comes to the prophet; I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will answer him that comes according to the multitude of his idols;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> That I may take the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in their own heart, because they are all estranged from me through their idols.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Therefore say unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Repent, and turn &#91;yourselves&#93; from your idols; and turn away your faces from all your abominations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> For every one of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, or of the stranger that sojourns in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which separates himself from me, and sets up his idols in his heart, and puts the stumblingblock of his iniquity before his face, and comes to a prophet to enquire of him concerning me; I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will answer him by myself:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And I will set my face against that man, and will make him a sign and a proverb, and I will cut him off from the midst of my people; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And if the prophet be deceived when he has spoken a thing, I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have deceived that prophet, and I will stretch out my hand upon him, and will destroy him from the midst of my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> And they shall bear the punishment of their iniquity: the punishment of the prophet shall be even as the punishment of him that seeks &#91;unto him&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> That the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; may go no more astray from me, neither be polluted any more with all their transgressions; but that they may be my people, and I may be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came again to me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Son of man, when the land sins against me by trespassing grievously, then will I stretch out my hand upon it, and will break the staff of the bread thereof, and will send famine upon it, and will cut off man and beast from it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Though these three men, Noach &#91;rest&#93;, Dani’el &#91;God is Judge&#93;, and Job, were in it, they should deliver &#91;but&#93; their own souls by their righteousness, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> If I cause destructive (hurtful; noxious) beasts to pass through the land, and they spoil (plunder; prey; booty) it, so that it be desolate, that no man may pass through because of the beasts:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> &#91;Though&#93; these three men &#91;were&#93; in it, &#91;as&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), they shall deliver neither sons nor daughters; they only shall be delivered, but the land shall be desolate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Or &#91;if&#93; I bring a sword upon that land, and say, Sword, go through the land; so that I cut off man and beast from it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> Though these three men &#91;were&#93; in it, &#91;as&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), they shall deliver neither sons nor daughters, but they only shall be delivered themselves.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> Or &#91;if&#93; I send a pestilence into that land, and pour out my fury upon it in blood, to cut off from it man and beast:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> Though No’ah &#91;Motion&#93;, Dani’el &#91;God is Judge&#93;, and Job, &#91;were&#93; in it, &#91;as&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), they shall deliver neither son nor daughter; they shall &#91;but&#93; deliver their own souls by their righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); How much more when I send my four sore judgments upon Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, the sword, and the famine, and the destructive (hurtful; noxious) beast, and the pestilence, to cut off from it man and beast?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Yet, behold, in it shall be left a remnant that shall be brought forth, &#91;both&#93; sons and daughters: behold, they shall come forth unto you, and you2f shall see their way and their doings: and you2f shall be comforted concerning the evil that I have brought upon Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, &#91;even&#93; concerning all that I have brought upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> And they shall comfort you, when you2f see their ways and their doings: and you2f shall know that I have not done without cause all that I have done in it, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, What is the vine tree more than any tree, &#91;or than&#93; a branch which is among the trees of the forest?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Shall wood be taken thereof to do any work? or will &#91;men&#93; take a pin of it to hang any vessel thereon?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Behold, it is cast into the fire for fuel; the fire devours both the ends of it, and the midst of it is burned. Is it meet for &#91;any&#93; work?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Behold, when it was whole, it was meet for no work: how much less shall it be meet yet for &#91;any&#93; work, when the fire has devoured it, and it is burned?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); As the vine tree among the trees of the forest, which I have given to the fire for fuel, so will I give the inhabitants of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And I will set my face against them; they shall go out from &#91;one&#93; fire, and &#91;another&#93; fire shall devour them; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I set my face against them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And I will make the land desolate, because they have committed a trespass, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Again the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, cause Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; to know her abominations,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) unto Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;; You1s birth and youi nativity &#91;is&#93; of the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;; youi father &#91;was&#93; an Emori &#91;Rebellious Highlanders&#93;, and youi mother an Hitti &#91;Terrorists&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> And &#91;as for&#93; youri nativity, in the day youi were born youri navel was not cut, neither were youi washed in water to supple &#91;youi&#93;; youi were not salted at all, nor swaddled at all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> None eye pitied youi, to do any of these unto youi, to have compassion upon youi; but youi were cast out in the open field, to the loathing of youi person, in the day that youi were born.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And when I passed by youi, and saw youi polluted in youri own blood, I said unto youi &#91;when youi were&#93; in youri blood, Live; yes, I said unto youi &#91;when youi were&#93; in youri blood, Live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> I have caused youi to multiply as the bud of the field, and youi have increased and waxen great, and youi are come to excellent ornaments: &#91;youri&#93; breasts are fashioned, and youri hair is grown, whereas youi &#91;were&#93; naked and bare.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Now when I passed by youi, and looked upon youi, behold, youri time &#91;was&#93; the time of love; and I spread my skirt over youi, and covered youri nakedness: yes, I swore unto youi, and entered into a covenant with youi, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), and youi became mine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Then washed I youi with water; yes, I thoroughly washed away youri blood from youi, and I anointed youi with oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> I clothed youi also with adorned with needlework work, and shod youi with badgers’ skin, and I girded youi about with fine linen, and I covered youi with silk.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> I decked youi also with ornaments, and I put bracelets upon youri hands, and a chain on youri neck.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And I put a jewel on youri forehead, and earrings in youri ears, and a beautiful crown upon youri head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Thus were youi decked with gold and silver; and youri clothing &#91;was of&#93; fine linen, and silk, and adorned with needlework work; youi did eat fine flour, and honey, and oil: and youi were exceeding beautiful, and youi did prosper into a kingdom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And youri renown (great reputation; being well-known) went forth among the heathen for youri beauty: for it &#91;was&#93; perfect through my comeliness, which I had put upon youi, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> But youi did trust in youri own beauty, and played the prostitute because of youri renown (great reputation; being well-known), and poured out youri fornications on everyone that passed by; his it was.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> And of youri garments youi did take, and decked youi high places with diverse colours, and played the prostitute thereupon: &#91;the like things&#93; shall not come, neither shall it be &#91;so&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Youi have also taken youri fair jewels of my gold and of my silver, which I had given youi, and made to yourself images of men, and did commit whoredom with them,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> And took youri embroidered garments, and covered them: and youi have set my oil and my incense before them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> My meat also which I gave youi, fine flour, and oil, and honey, &#91;which&#93; I fed youi, youi have even set it before them for a soothing aroma: and &#91;thus&#93; it was, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> Moreover youi have taken youri sons and youri daughters, whom youi have borne unto me, and these have youi sacrificed unto them to be devoured. &#91;Is this&#93; of youri cult prostitution a small matter,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> That youi have slain my children, and delivered them to cause them to pass through &#91;the fire&#93; for them?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> And in all youri abominations and youri cult prostitution youi have not remembered the days of youri youth, when youi were naked and bare, &#91;and&#93; were polluted in youri blood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> And it came to pass after all youri wickedness, (woe, woe unto youi! says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER);)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> &#91;That&#93; youi have also built unto youi an eminent place, and have made youi an high place in every street.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Youi have built youri high place at every head of the way, and have made youri beauty to be abhorred, and have opened youri legs to everyone that passed by, and multiplied youri cult prostitution.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> Youi have also committed fornication with the Mitzri youri neighbours, great of flesh; and have increased youri cult prostitution, to provoke me to anger.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Behold, therefore I have stretched out my hand over youi, and have diminished youri ordinary &#91;food&#93;, and delivered youi unto the will of them that hate youi, the daughters of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, which are ashamed of youri lewd way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> Youi have played the whore also with the children of Ashur, because youi were insatiable; yes, youi have played the prostitute with them, and yet could not be satisfied.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Youi have moreover multiplied youri fornication in the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93; unto Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;; and yet youi were not satisfied with this!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> How weak is youri heart, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), seeing youi do all these &#91;things&#93;, the work of an shameless whorish woman;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> In that youi build youri eminent place in the head of every way, and makes youri high place in every street; and have not been as an prostitute, in that youi scorn getting paid;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> &#91;But as&#93; a wife that commits adultery, &#91;which&#93; takes strangers instead of her husband!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> They give gifts to all whores: but youi give youri gifts to all youri lovers, and hire them, that they may come unto youi on every side for youri whoredom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And the contrary is in youi from &#91;other&#93; women in youri cult prostitution, whereas none follows youi to commit cult prostitution: and in that youi give a reward, and no reward is given unto youi, therefore youi are contrary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> For this reason, O prostitute, hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youi filthiness was poured out, and youri nakedness discovered through youri cult prostitution with youri lovers, and with all the idols of youri abominations, and by the blood of youri children, which youi did give unto them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Behold, therefore I will gather all youri lovers, with whom youi have taken pleasure, and all &#91;them&#93; that youi have loved, with all &#91;them&#93; that youi have hated; I will even gather them roundabout against youi, and will expose youri nakedness unto them, that they may see all youri nakedness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And I will judge youi, as women that breaks wedlock and sheds blood are judged; and I will give youi blood in fury and jealousy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And I will also give youi into their hand, and they shall throw down youri eminent place, and shall break down youri high places: they shall strip youi also of youri clothes, and shall take youri fair jewels, and leave youri naked and bare.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> They shall also bring up a company against youi, and they shall stone youi with stones, and thrust youi through with their swords.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And they shall burn youri houses with fire, and execute judgments upon youi in the sight of many women: and I will cause youi to cease from playing the prostitute, and youi also shall give no pay (to your lovers) any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> So will I make my fury toward youi to rest, and my jealousy shall depart from youi, and I will be quiet, and will be no more angry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Because youi have not remembered the days of youri youth, but have fretted me in all these &#91;things&#93;; behold, therefore I also will recompense youri way upon &#91;youi&#93; head, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER): and youi shall not commit this lewdness above all youri abominations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> Behold, everyone that uses proverbs shall use &#91;this&#93; proverb against youi, saying, As &#91;is&#93; the mother, &#91;so is&#93; her daughter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> Youi &#91;are&#93; youri mother’s daughter, that loathes her husband and her children; and youi &#91;are&#93; the sister of youri sisters, which loathed their husbands and their children: your mother &#91;was&#93; an Hitti &#91;Terrorists&#93;, and your father an Emori &#91;Rebellious Highlanders&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> And youri elder sister &#91;is&#93; Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93;, she and her daughters that dwell at youri left hand: and youri younger sister, that dwells at youri right hand, &#91;is&#93; S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; and her daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> Yet have youi not walked after their ways, nor done after their abominations: but, as &#91;if that were&#93; a very little &#91;thing&#93;, youi were corrupted more than they in all youri ways!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; youri sister has not done, she nor her daughters, as youi have done, youi and youri daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> Behold, this was the iniquity of youri sister S’dom &#91;Burning&#93;, pride, fullness of bread, and abundance of idleness was in her and in her daughters, neither did she strengthen the hand of the poor and needy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> And they were arrogant (proud; lifted up), and committed abomination before me: therefore I took them away as I saw &#91;good&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> Neither has Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93; committed half of youri sins; but youi have multiplied youri abominations more than they, and have justified youri sisters in all youri abominations which youi have done.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> Youi also, which have judged youri sisters, bear youri own shame for youri sins that youi have committed more abominable than they: they are more righteous than youi: yes, be youi confounded also, and bear youri shame, in that youi have justified youri sisters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> When I shall bring again their captivity, the captivity of S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; and her daughters, and the captivity of Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93; and her daughters, then &#91;will I bring again&#93; the captivity of youri captives in the midst of them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> That youi may bear youri own shame, and may be confounded in all that youi have done, in that youi are a comfort unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> When youri sisters, S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; and her daughters, shall return to their former estate, and Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93; and her daughters shall return to their former estate, then youi and youri daughters shall return to your former estate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> For youri sister S’dom &#91;Burning&#93; was not mentioned by youri mouth in the day of youri pride,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> Before youri wickedness was discovered, as at the time of &#91;youri&#93; reproach (disgrace; shame) of the daughters of Aram (Syria) &#91;exalted&#93;, and all &#91;that are&#93; round about her, the daughters of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, which despise youi round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58"> Youi have borne youri lewdness and youri abominations, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v59" data-v="59"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will even deal with youi as youi have done, which have despised the oath in breaking the Covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60"> Nevertheless I will remember my covenant with youi in the days of youri youth, and I will establish unto youi an everlasting covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v61" data-v="61"> Then youi shall remember youri ways, and be ashamed, when youi shall receive youri sisters, youri elder and youri younger: and I will give them unto youi for daughters, but not by youri covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62"> And I will establish my covenant with youi; and youi shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v63" data-v="63"> That youi may remember, and be confounded, and never open youri mouth anymore because of youri shame, when I am pacified toward youi for all that youi have done, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, put forth a riddle, and speak a parable unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); A great eagle with great wings, longwinged, full of feathers, which had diverse colours, came unto L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, and took the highest branch of the cedar:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> He cropped off the top of his young twigs, and carried it into a land of traffick (trade; do business); he set it in a city of merchants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> He took also of the seed of the land, and planted it in a fruitful field; he placed &#91;it&#93; by great waters, &#91;and&#93; set it &#91;as&#93; a willow tree.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And it grew, and became a spreading vine of low stature, whose branches turned toward him, and the roots thereof were under him: so it became a vine, and brought forth branches, and shot forth sprigs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> There was also another great eagle with great wings and many feathers: and, behold, this vine did bend her roots toward him, and shot forth her branches toward him, that he might water it by the furrows of her plantation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> It was planted in a good soil by great waters, that it might bring forth branches, and that it might bear fruit, that it might be a goodly vine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Say youi, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Shall it prosper? shall he not pull up the roots thereof, and cut off the fruit thereof, that it wither? it shall wither in all the leaves of her spring, even without great power or many people to pluck it up by the roots thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Yes, behold, &#91;being&#93; planted, shall it prosper? shall it not utterly wither, when the east wind touches it? it shall wither in the furrows where it grew.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Say now to the rebellious house, Know you2f not what these &#91;things mean&#93;? tell &#91;them&#93;, Behold, the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; is come to Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and has taken the king thereof, and the princes thereof, and led them with him to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> And has taken of the king’s seed, and made a covenant with him, and has taken an oath of him: he has also taken the mighty of the land:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> That the kingdom might be base, that it might not lift itself up, &#91;but&#93; that by keeping of his covenant it might stand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> But he rebelled against him in sending his ambassadors into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, that they might give him horses and much people. Shall he prosper? shall he escape that does such &#91;things&#93;? or shall he break the covenant, and be delivered?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), surely in the place &#91;where&#93; the king &#91;dwells&#93; that made him king, whose oath he despised, and whose covenant he broke, &#91;even&#93; with him in the midst of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; he shall die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Neither shall Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; with &#91;his&#93; mighty army and great company make for him in the war, by casting up mounts, and building forts, to cut off many persons:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> Seeing he despised the oath by breaking the covenant, when, lo, he had given his hand, and has done all these &#91;things&#93;, he shall not escape.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); &#91;As&#93; I live, surely my oath that he has despised, and my covenant that he has broken, even it will I recompense upon his own head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> And I will spread my net upon him, and he shall be taken in my snare, and I will bring him to Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, and will plead with him there for his trespass that he has trespassed against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And all his fugitives with all his bands shall fall by the sword, and they that remain shall be scattered toward all winds: and you2f shall know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will also take of the highest branch of the high cedar, and will set &#91;it&#93;; I will crop off from the top of his young twigs a tender one, and will plant &#91;it&#93; upon an high mountain and eminent:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> In the mountain of the height of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; will I plant it: and it shall bring forth boughs, and bear fruit, and be a goodly cedar: and under it shall dwell all fowl of every wing; in the shadow of the branches thereof shall they dwell.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> And all the trees of the field shall know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have brought down the high tree, have exalted the low tree, have dried up the green tree, and have made the dry tree to flourish: I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken and have done &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me again, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> What mean you2f, that you2f use this proverb concerning the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, The fathers have eaten sour grapes, and the children’s teeth are set on edge?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), you2f shall not have &#91;occasion&#93; any more to use this proverb in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Behold, all souls are my; as the soul of the father, so also the soul of the son is my: the soul that sins, it shall die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> But if a man be just, and do that which is lawful and right,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> &#91;And&#93; has not eaten upon the mountains, neither has lifted up his eyes to the idols of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, neither has defiled his neighbour’s wife, neither has come near to a monstrous woman,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And has not oppressed any, &#91;but&#93; has restored to the debtor his pledge, has spoiled none by violence, has given his bread to the hungry, and has covered the naked with a garment;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> He &#91;that&#93; has not given forth upon usury (interest paid for using money), neither has taken any increase, &#91;that&#93; has withdrawn his hand from iniquity, has executed true judgment between man and man,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Has walked in my statutes, and has kept my judgments, to deal truly; he &#91;is&#93; just, he shall surely live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> If he beget (to be father of; be ancestor of) a son &#91;that is&#93; a robber, a shedder of blood, and &#91;that&#93; does the like to &#91;any&#93; one of these &#91;things&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And that does not any of those &#91;duties&#93;, but even has eaten upon the mountains, and defiled his neighbour’s wife,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Has oppressed the poor and needy, has spoiled by violence, has not restored the pledge, and has lifted up his eyes to the idols, has committed abomination,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Has given forth upon usury (interest paid for using money), and has taken increase: shall he then live? he shall not live: he has done all these abominations; he shall surely die; his blood shall be upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Now, lo, &#91;if&#93; he beget (to be father of; be ancestor of) a son, that sees all his father’s sins which he has done, and considers, and does not such like,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> &#91;That&#93; has not eaten upon the mountains, neither has lifted up his eyes to the idols of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, has not defiled his neighbour’s wife,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Neither has oppressed any, has not withheld the pledge, neither has spoiled by violence, &#91;but&#93; has given his bread to the hungry, and has covered the naked with a garment,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> &#91;That&#93; has taken off his hand from the poor, &#91;that&#93; has not received usury (interest paid for using money) nor increase, has executed my judgments, has walked in my statutes; he shall not die for the iniquity of his father, he shall surely live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> &#91;As for&#93; his father, because he cruelly oppressed, spoiled his brother by violence, and did &#91;that&#93; which &#91;is&#93; not good among his people, lo, even he shall die in his iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> Yet say you2f, Why? does not the son bear the iniquity of the father? When the son has done that which is lawful and right, &#91;and&#93; has kept all my statutes, and has done them, he shall surely live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> The soul that sins, it shall die. The son shall not bear the iniquity of the father, neither shall the father bear the iniquity of the son: the righteousness of the righteous shall be upon him, and the wickedness of the wicked shall be upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> But if the wicked will turn from all his sins that he has committed, and keep all my statutes, and do that which is lawful and right, he shall surely live, he shall not die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> All his transgressions that he has committed, they shall not be mentioned unto him: in his righteousness that he has done he shall live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Have I any pleasure at all that the wicked should die? says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER): &#91;and&#93; not that he should return from his ways, and live?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> But when the righteous turns away from his righteousness, and commit iniquity, &#91;and&#93; does according to all the abominations that the wicked &#91;man&#93; does, shall he live? All his righteousness that he has done shall not be mentioned: in his trespass that he has trespassed, and in his sin that he has sinned, in them shall he die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Yet you2f say, The way of the Lord is not equal. Hear now, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Is not my way equal? are not your ways unequal?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> When a righteous &#91;man&#93; turns away from his righteousness, and commit iniquity, and dies in them; for his iniquity that he has done shall he die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Again, when the wicked &#91;man&#93; turns away from his wickedness that he has committed, and does that which is lawful and right, he shall save his soul alive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> Because he considers, and turns away from all his transgressions that he has committed, he shall surely live, he shall not die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Yet says the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, The way of the Lord is not equal. O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, are not my ways equal? are not your ways unequal?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Therefore I will judge you, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, everyone according to his ways, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER). Repent, and turn &#91;yourselves&#93; from all your transgressions; so iniquity shall not be your ruin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Cast away from you all your transgressions, whereby you2f have transgressed; and make you a new heart and a new spirit: for why will you2f die, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> For I have no pleasure in the death of him that dies, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER): wherefore turn &#91;yourselves&#93;, and live you2f.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Moreover take youi up a lamentation for the princes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> And say, What &#91;is&#93; youri mother? A lioness: she lay down among lions, she nourished her whelps among young lions.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And she brought up one of her whelps: it became a young lion, and it learned to catch the prey; it devoured men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> The nations also heard of him; he was taken in their pit, and they brought him with chains unto the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Now when she saw that she had waited, &#91;and&#93; her hope was lost, then she took another of her whelps, &#91;and&#93; made him a young lion.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And he went up and down among the lions, he became a young lion, and learned to catch the prey, &#91;and&#93; devoured men.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And they put him in ward (in custody, with a guard; prison) in chains, and brought him to the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;: they brought him into holds, that his voice should no more be heard upon the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And she had strong rods for the scepters of them that bore rule, and her stature was exalted among the thick branches, and she appeared in her height with the multitude of her branches.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> But she was plucked up in fury, she was cast down to the ground, and the east wind dried up her fruit: her strong rods were broken and withered; the fire consumed them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And fire is gone out of a rod of her branches, &#91;which&#93; has devoured her fruit, so that she has no strong rod &#91;to be&#93; a scepter to rule. This &#91;is&#93; a lamentation, and shall be for a lamentation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And it came to pass in the seventh year, in the fifth &#91;month&#93;, the tenth &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; certain of the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; came to enquire of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and sat before me.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Then came the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Son of man, speak unto the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Are you2f come to enquire of me? &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), I will not be enquired of by you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Will youi judge them, Son of man, will youi judge &#91;them&#93;? cause them to know the abominations of their fathers:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> And say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); In the day when I chose Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and lifted up my hand unto the seed of the house of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and made myself known unto them in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, when I lifted up my hand unto them, saying, I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> In the day &#91;that&#93; I lifted up my hand unto them, to bring them forth of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; into a land that I had discovered (spied out; examined) for them, flowing with milk and honey, which &#91;is&#93; the glory of all lands:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Then said I unto them, Cast you2f away every man the abominations of his eyes, and defile not yourselves with the idols of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> But they rebelled against me, and would not hearken unto me: they did not every man cast away the abominations of their eyes, neither did they forsake the idols of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: then I said, I will pour out my fury upon them, to accomplish my anger against them in the midst of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> But I wrought for my name’s sake, that it should not be polluted before the heathen, among whom they &#91;were&#93;, in whose sight I made myself known unto them, in bringing them forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And I gave them my statutes, and showed them my judgments, which &#91;if&#93; a man do, he shall even live in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Moreover also I gave them my Shabbat, to be a sign between me and them, that they might know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> But the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; rebelled against me in the wilderness: they walked not in my statutes, and they despised my judgments, which &#91;if&#93; a man do, he shall even live in them; and my Shabbat they greatly polluted: then I said, I would pour out my fury upon them in the wilderness, to consume them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> But I wrought for my name’s sake, that it should not be polluted before the heathen, in whose sight I brought them out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Yet also I lifted up my hand unto them in the wilderness, that I would not bring them into the land which I had given &#91;them&#93;, flowing with milk and honey, which &#91;is&#93; the glory of all lands;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Because they despised my judgments, and walked not in my statutes, but polluted my Shabbat: for their heart went after their idols.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> But I said unto their children in the wilderness, Walk you2f not in the statutes of your fathers, neither observe their judgments, nor defile yourselves with their idols:</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> And hallow (to approve, commend, accept) my Shabbat; and they shall be a sign between me and you, that you2f may know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> Yet the children rebelled against me: they walked not in my statutes, neither kept my judgments to do them, which &#91;if&#93; a man do, he shall even live in them; they polluted my Shabbat: then I said, I would pour out my fury upon them, to accomplish my anger against them in the wilderness.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Nevertheless I withdrew my hand, and wrought for my name’s sake, that it should not be polluted in the sight of the heathen, in whose sight I brought them forth.</span>

<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> Because they had not executed my judgments, but had despised my statutes, and had polluted my Shabbat, and their eyes were after their fathers’ idols.</span>

<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Wherefore I permitted them, or gave them over to, &#91;false&#93; statutes that are not good, and judgments whereby they should not live.;</span>
