<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> And I polluted them in their own gifts, in that they caused to pass through &#91;the fire&#93; all that opens the womb, that I might make them desolate, to the end that they might know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Therefore, Son of man, speak unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Yet in this your fathers have blasphemed me, in that they have committed a trespass against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> &#91;For&#93; when I had brought them into the land, &#91;for&#93; the which I lifted up my hand to give it to them, then they saw every high hill, and all the thick trees, and they offered there their sacrifices, and there they presented the provocation of their offering: there also they made their soothing aroma, and poured out there their drink offerings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Then I said unto them, What &#91;is&#93; the high place whereunto you2f go? And the name thereof is called Bamah &#91;high place&#93; unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> For what reason say unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Are you2f polluted after the manner of your fathers? and commit you2f whoredom after their abominations?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> For when you2f offer your gifts, when you2f make your sons to pass through the fire, you2f pollute yourselves with all your idols, even unto this day: and shall I be enquired of by you, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;? &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), I will not be enquired of by you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And that which comes into your mind shall not be at all, that you2f say, We will be as the heathen, as the families of the countries, to serve wood and stone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), surely with a mighty hand, and with a stretched out arm, and with fury poured out, will I rule over you:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And I will bring you out from the people, and will gather you out of the countries wherein you2f are scattered, with a mighty hand, and with a stretched out arm, and with fury poured out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And I will bring you into the wilderness of the people, and there will I plead with you face to face.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Like as I pleaded with your fathers in the wilderness of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, so will I plead with you, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And I will cause you to pass under the rod, and I will bring you into the bond of the covenant:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And I will purge out from among you the rebels, and them that transgress against me: I will bring them forth out of the country where they sojourn, and they shall not enter into the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> As for you, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Go you2f, serve you2f everyone his idols, and hereafter &#91;also&#93;, if you2f will not hearken unto me: but pollute you2f my holy name no more with your gifts, and with your idols.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> For in my holy mountain, in the mountain of the height of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), there shall all the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, all of them in the land, serve me: there will I accept them, and there will I require your offerings, and the firstfruits of your oblations, with all your holy things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> I will accept you with your soothing aroma, when I bring you out from the people, and gather you out of the countries wherein you2f have been scattered; and I will be sanctified in you before the heathen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I shall bring you into the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, into the country &#91;for&#93; the which I lifted up my hand to give it to your fathers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And there shall you2f remember your ways, and all your doings, wherein you2f have been defiled; and you2f shall loathe yourselves in your own sight for all your evils that you2f have committed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I have wrought with you for my name’s sake, not according to your wicked ways, nor according to your corrupt doings, O you2f house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> Son of man, set youi face toward the south, and drop &#91;youi word&#93; toward the south, and prophesy against the forest of the south field;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And say to the forest of the south, Hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will kindle a fire in youi, and it shall devour every green tree in youi, and every dry tree: the flaming flame shall not be quenched, and all faces from the south to the north shall be burned in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And all flesh shall see that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have kindled it: it shall not be quenched.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> Then said I, Ah Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER)! they say of me, Does he not speak parables?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, set youri face toward Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, and drop &#91;youi word&#93; toward the holy places, and prophesy against the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say to the and of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against youi, and will draw forth my sword out of his sheath, and will cut off from youi the righteous and the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Seeing then that I will cut off from youi the righteous and the wicked, therefore shall my sword go forth out of his sheath against all flesh from the south to the north:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> That all flesh may know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have drawn forth my sword out of his sheath: it shall not return any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Sigh therefore, youi Son of man, with the breaking of &#91;youri&#93; waist; and with bitterness sigh before their eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And it shall be, when they say unto youi, For what reason sigh youi? that youi shall answer, For the tidings; because it comes: and every heart shall melt, and all hands shall be feeble, and every spirit shall faint, and all knees shall be weak &#91;as&#93; water: behold, it comes, and shall be brought to pass, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Again the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Son of man, prophesy, and say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;; Say, A sword, a sword is sharpened, and also furbished:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> It is sharpened to make a sore slaughter; it is furbished that it may glitter: should we then make gladness? it scorns the rod of my son, &#91;as&#93; every tree.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And he has given it to be furbished, that it may be handled: this sword is sharpened, and it is furbished, to give it into the hand of the slayer.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Cry and howl, Son of man: for it shall be upon my people, it &#91;shall be&#93; upon all the princes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: terrors by reason of the sword shall be upon my people: strike therefore upon &#91;youi&#93; thigh.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> The children of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity), and all the Kasdim &#91;clod-breakers&#93;, P’kod &#91;visitation&#93;, and Shoa &#91;rich&#93;, and Koa &#91;he-camel&#93;, &#91;and&#93; all the children of Ashur with them: all of them desirable young men, captains and rulers, great lords and renowned, all of them riding upon horses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> And they shall come against youi with chariots, wagons, and wheels, and with an assembly of people, &#91;which&#93; shall set against youri buckler (round shielf held with a grip) and shield and helmet round about: and I will set judgment before them, and they shall judge youi according to their judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> And I will set my jealousy against youi, and they shall deal furiously with youi: they shall take away youri nose and youri ears; and youri remnant shall fall by the sword: they shall take youri sons and youri daughters; and youri residue (part that remains, the rest) shall be devoured by the fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> They shall also strip youi out of youri clothes, and take away youri fair jewels.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Thus will I make youri lewdness to cease from youi, and youri whoredom &#91;brought&#93; from the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: so that youi shall not lift up youri eyes unto them, nor remember Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will deliver youi into the hand &#91;of them&#93; whom youi hate, into the hand &#91;of them&#93; from whom youri mind is alienated:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And they shall deal with youi hatefully, and shall take away all youri labour, and shall leave youi naked and bare: and the nakedness of youri cult prostitution shall be discovered, both youri lewdness and youri cult prostitution.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> I will do these &#91;things&#93; unto youi, because youi have gone a whoring after the heathen, &#91;and&#93; because youi are polluted with their idols.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Youi have walked in the way of youri sister; therefore will I give her cup into youri hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); youi shall drink of youri sister’s cup deep and large: youi shall be laughed to scorn and had in ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats); it contains much.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> youi shall be filled with drunkenness and sorrow, with the cup of astonishment and desolation, with the cup of youri sister Shomron &#91;watch mountain&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> youi shall even drink it and suck &#91;it&#93; out, and youi shall break the shards thereof, and pluck off youri own breasts: for I have spoken &#91;it&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youi have forgotten me, and cast me behind youri back, therefore bear youi also youri lewdness and youri cult prostitution.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said moreover unto me; Son of man, will youi judge Oholah &#91;her own tent&#93; and Oholivah &#91;woman of the tent&#93;? yes, declare unto them their abominations;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> That they have committed adultery, and blood &#91;is&#93; in their hands, and with their idols have they committed adultery, and have also caused their sons, whom they bore unto me, to pass for them through &#91;the fire&#93;, to devour &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> Moreover this they have done unto me: they have defiled my Sanctuary in the same day, and have profaned my Shabbat.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> For when they had slain their children to their idols, then they came the same day into my sanctuary to Profane (treat as common; defile) it; and, lo, thus have they done in the midst of my house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And furthermore, that you2f have sent for men to come from far, unto whom a messenger &#91;was&#93; sent; and, lo, they came: for whom youi did wash yourself, painted youri eyes, and decked yourself with ornaments,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And sat upon a stately bed, and a table prepared before it, whereupon youi have set my incense and my oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And a voice of a multitude being at ease &#91;was&#93; with her: and with the men of the common sort &#91;were&#93; brought S’va &#91;drunkards&#93; from the wilderness, which put bracelets upon their hands, and beautiful crowns upon their heads.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Then said I unto &#91;her that was&#93; old in adulteries, Will they now commit cult prostitution with her, and she &#91;with them&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> Yet they went in unto her, as they go in unto a woman that plays the prostitute: so went they in unto Oholah &#91;her own tent&#93; and unto Oholivah &#91;woman of the tent&#93;, the lewd women.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And the righteous men, they shall judge them after the manner of adulteresses, and after the manner of women that shed blood; because they &#91;are&#93; adulteresses, and blood &#91;is&#93; in their hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will bring up a company upon them, and will give them to be removed and spoiled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And the company shall stone them with stones, and dispatch them with their swords; they shall slay their sons and their daughters, and burn up their houses with fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> Thus will I cause lewdness to cease out of the land, that all women may be taught not to do after your lewdness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> And they shall recompense your lewdness upon you, and you2f shall bear the sins of your idols: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Again in the ninth year, in the tenth month, in the tenth &#91;day&#93; of the month, the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, write youi the name of the day, &#91;even&#93; of this same day: the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; set himself against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; this same day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And utter a parable unto the rebellious house, and say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Set on a pot, set &#91;it&#93; on, and also pour water into it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Gather the pieces thereof into it, &#91;even&#93; every good piece, the thigh, and the shoulder; fill &#91;it&#93; with the choice bones.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Take the choice of the flock, and burn also the bones under it, &#91;and&#93; make it boil well, and let them seethe the bones of it in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> For what reason thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Woe to the bloody city, to the pot whose scum &#91;is&#93; in it, and whose scum is not gone out of it! bring it out piece by piece; let no lot fall upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> For her blood is in the midst of her; she set it upon the top of a rock; she poured it not upon the ground, to cover it with dust;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> That it might cause fury to come up to take vengeance; I have set her blood upon the top of a rock, that it should not be covered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Woe to the bloody city! I will even make the pile for fire great.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Heap on wood, kindle the fire, consume the flesh, and spice it well, and let the bones be burned.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Then set it empty upon the coals thereof, that the brass of it may be hot, and may burn, and &#91;that&#93; the filthiness of it may be molten in it, &#91;that&#93; the scum of it may be consumed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> She has wearied &#91;herself&#93; with lies, and her great scum went not forth out of her: her scum &#91;shall be&#93; in the fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> In youri filthiness &#91;is&#93; lewdness: because I have purged youi, and youi were not purged, youi shall not be purged from youri filthiness any more, till I have caused my fury to rest upon youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;: it shall come to pass, and I will do &#91;it&#93;; I will not go back, neither will I spare, neither will I repent; according to youri ways, and according to youri doings, shall they judge youi, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Also the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Son of man, behold, I take away from youi the desire of youri eyes with a stroke: yet neither shall youi mourn nor weep, neither shall youri tears run down.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Refrain (restrain; refrein from acting) to cry, make no mourning for the dead, bind the tire of youri head upon youi, and put on youri shoes upon youri feet, and cover not &#91;youri&#93; lips, and eat not the bread of men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> So I spoke unto the people in the morning: and at even my wife died; and I did in the morning as I was commanded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And the people said unto me, Will youi not tell us what these &#91;things are&#93; to us, that youi do &#91;so&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> Then I answered them, The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> Speak unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will Profane (treat as common; defile) my sanctuary, the excellency of your strength, the desire of your eyes, and that which your soul pities; and your sons and your daughters whom you2f have left shall fall by the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> And you2f shall do as I have done: you2f shall not cover &#91;your&#93; lips, nor eat the bread of men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> And your tires &#91;shall be&#93; upon your heads, and your shoes upon your feet: you2f shall not mourn nor weep; but you2f shall pine away for your iniquities, and mourn one toward another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> Thus Yechezk’el &#91;God strengthens&#93; is unto you a sign: according to all that he has done shall you2f do: and when this comes, you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Also, youi Son of man, &#91;shall it&#93; not &#91;be&#93; in the day when I take from them their strength, the joy of their glory, the desire of their eyes, and that whereupon they set their minds, their sons and their daughters,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> &#91;That&#93; he that escapes in that day shall come unto youi, to cause &#91;youi&#93; to hear &#91;it&#93; with &#91;youri&#93; ears?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> In that day shall youri mouth be opened to him which is escaped, and youi shall speak, and be no more dumb: and youi shall be a sign unto them; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came again unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, set youri face against the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, and prophesy against them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say unto the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, Hear the word of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youi said, Aha, against my sanctuary, when it was profaned; and against the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when it was desolate; and against the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, when they went into captivity;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Behold, therefore I will deliver youi to the men of the east for a possession, and they shall set their palaces in youi, and make their dwellings in youi: they shall eat youri fruit, and they shall drink youri milk.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> And I will make Rabbah &#91;Great&#93; a stable for camels, and the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; a couchingplace for flocks: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youi have clapped &#91;youri&#93; hands, and stamped with the feet, and rejoiced in heart with all youri despite (contempt; angy hatred) against the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Behold, therefore I will stretch out my hand upon youi, and will deliver youi for a spoil (plunder; prey; booty) to the heathen; and I will cut youi off from the people, and I will cause youi to perish out of the countries: I will destroy youi; and youi shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because that Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; and Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93; do say, Behold, the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; &#91;is&#93; like unto all the heathen;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Therefore, behold, I will open the side of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; from the cities, from his cities &#91;which are&#93; on his frontiers, the glory of the country, Beit-Yeshimot &#91;House of the Desolation&#93;, Ba’al-M’on &#91;lord of the Habitation&#93;, and Kiryatayim &#91;two cities&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Unto the men of the east with the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, and will give them in possession, that the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; may not be remembered among the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And I will execute judgments upon Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because that Edom &#91;Red&#93; has dealt against the house of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; by taking vengeance, and has greatly offended, and revenged himself upon them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will also stretch out my hand upon Edom &#91;Red&#93;, and will cut off man and beast from it; and I will make it desolate from Teman &#91;south&#93;; and they of D’dan &#91;low country&#93; shall fall by the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And I will lay my vengeance upon Edom &#91;Red&#93; by the hand of my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and they shall do in Edom &#91;Red&#93; according to my anger and according to my fury; and they shall know my vengeance, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93; have dealt by revenge, and have taken vengeance with a despiteful heart, to destroy &#91;it&#93; for the old hatred;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will stretch out my hand upon the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, and I will cut off the K’reti &#91;executioners&#93;, and destroy the remnant of the sea coast.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> And I will execute great vengeance upon them with furious rebukes; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I shall lay my vengeance upon them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And it came to pass in the eleventh year, in the first &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, because that Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93; has said against Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93;, Aha, she is broken &#91;that was&#93; the gates of the people: she is turned unto me: I shall be replenished, &#91;now&#93; she is laid waste:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against youi, O Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, and will cause many nations to come up against youi, as the sea causes his waves to come up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> And they shall destroy the walls of Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, and break down her towers: I will also scrape her dust from her, and make her like the top of a rock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> It shall be &#91;a place for&#93; the spreading of nets in the midst of the sea: for I have spoken &#91;it&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER): and it shall become a spoil (plunder; prey; booty) to the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And her daughters which &#91;are&#93; in the field shall be slain by the sword; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will bring upon Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93; N’vukhadretzar &#91;may Nebo protect the crown&#93; king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93;, a king of kings, from the north, with horses, and with chariots, and with horsemen, and companies, and much people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> He shall slay with the sword youri daughters in the field: and he shall make a fort against youi, and cast a mount against youi, and lift up the buckler (round shielf held with a grip) against youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> And he shall set engines of war against youi walls, and with his axes he shall break down youri towers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> By reason of the abundance of his horses their dust shall cover youi: youri walls shall shake at the noise of the horsemen, and of the wheels, and of the chariots, when he shall enter into youri gates, as men enter into a city wherein is made a breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> With the hoofs of his horses shall he trample down all youri streets: he shall slay youri people by the sword, and youri strong garrisons shall go down to the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And they shall make a spoil (plunder; prey; booty) of youri riches, and make a prey of youri merchandise: and they shall break down youri walls, and destroy youi pleasant houses: and they shall lay youri stones and youri timber and youri dust in the midst of the water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> And I will cause the noise of youri songs to cease; and the sound of youri harps shall be no more heard.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And I will make youi like the top of a rock: youi shall be &#91;a place&#93; to spread nets upon; youi shall be built no more: for I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) to Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;; Shall not the isles shake at the sound of youri fall, when the wounded cry, when the slaughter is made in the midst of youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Then all the princes of the sea shall come down from their thrones, and lay away their robes, and put off their adorned with needlework garments: they shall clothe themselves with trembling; they shall sit upon the ground, and shall tremble at &#91;every&#93; moment, and be astonished at youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> And they shall take up a lamentation for youi, and say to youi, How are youi destroyed, &#91;that were&#93; inhabited of seafaring men, the renowned city, which were strong in the sea, she and her inhabitants, which cause their terror &#91;to be&#93; on all that haunt (where one goes freequently) it!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> Now shall the isles tremble in the day of youri fall; yes, the isles that &#91;are&#93; in the sea shall be troubled at youri departure.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); When I shall make youi a desolate city, like the cities that are not inhabited; when I shall bring up the deep upon youi, and great waters shall cover youi;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> When I shall bring youi down with them that descend into the pit, with the people of old time, and shall set youi in the low parts of the earth, in places desolate of old, with them that go down to the pit, that youi be not inhabited; and I shall set glory in the land of the living;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> I will make youi a terror, and youi &#91;shall be&#93; no &#91;more&#93;: though youi be sought for, yet shall youi never be found again, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came again unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Now, youi Son of man, take up a lamentation for Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say unto Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, O youi that are situate at the entry of the sea, &#91;which are&#93; a merchant of the people for many isles, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); O Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, youi have said, I &#91;am&#93; of perfect beauty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Your1s borders &#91;are&#93; in the midst of the seas, youri builders have perfected youri beauty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> They have made all youri &#91;ship&#93; boards of fir trees of S’nir &#91;snow mountain&#93;: they have taken cedars from L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93; to make masts for youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> &#91;Of&#93; the oaks of Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93; have they made youri oars; the company of the Ashuri &#91;guided; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous)&#93; have made youri benches &#91;of&#93; ivory, &#91;brought&#93; out of the isles of Kittim &#91;Bruisers&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Fine linen with adorned with needlework work from Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; was that which youi spread forth to be youri sail; blue and purple from the isles of Elishah &#91;God of the coming one&#93; was that which covered youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> The inhabitants of Tzidon &#91;Hunting&#93; and Arvad &#91;I shall break loose&#93; were youri mariners: youri wise &#91;men&#93;, O Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, &#91;that&#93; were in youi, were youri pilots.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> The ancients of G’val &#91;mountain&#93; and the wise &#91;men&#93; thereof were in youi youri caulkers: all the ships of the sea with their mariners were in youi to occupy youri merchandise.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> They of Paras (Persia) &#91;pure or splendid&#93; and of Lud &#91;strife&#93; and of Put &#91;a bow&#93; were in youi army, youri men of war: they hanged the shield and helmet in youi; they set forth youri comeliness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> The men of Arvad &#91;I shall break loose&#93; with youri army &#91;were&#93; upon youri walls round about, and the Gamadim &#91;warriors&#93; were in youri towers: they hanged their shields upon youri walls round about; they have made youri beauty perfect.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93; &#91;was&#93; youri merchant by reason of the multitude of all &#91;kind of&#93; riches; with silver, iron, tin, and lead, they traded in youri wares (something gotten at the fair).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Yavan (Greece) &#91;Ionia&#93;, Tuval &#91;you shall be brought&#93;, and Meshekh &#91;drawing out&#93;, they &#91;were&#93; youri merchants: they traded the persons of men and vessels of brass in youri market.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> They of the house of Togarmah &#91;you will break her&#93; traded in youri wares (something gotten at the fair) with horses and horsemen and mules.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> The men of D’dan &#91;low country&#93; &#91;were&#93; youri merchants; many isles &#91;were&#93; the merchandise of youi hand: they brought youi &#91;for&#93; a present horns of ivory and ebony.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Aram (Syria) &#91;exalted&#93; &#91;was&#93; youri merchant by reason of the multitude of the wares of youri making: they occupied in youri wares (something gotten at the fair) with emeralds, purple, and adorned with needlework work, and fine linen, and coral, and agate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, they &#91;were&#93; youri merchants: they traded in youri market wheat of Minnit &#91;distribution&#93;, and cakes, and honey, and oil, and balm (a medicine; rosin from a bush).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> Dammesek (Damascus) &#91;silent is the sackcloth weaver&#93; &#91;was&#93; youri merchant in the multitude of the wares of youri making, for the multitude of all riches; in the wine of Helbon &#91;fertile&#93;, and white wool.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> Dan &#91;Judge&#93; also and Yavan (Greece) &#91;Ionia&#93; going to and fro occupied in youri wares (something gotten at the fair): bright iron, cassia, and calamus, were in youri market.</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> Arabia &#91;steppe-dwellers&#93;, and all the princes of Kedar &#91;dark&#93;, they occupied with youri in lambs, and rams, and goats: in these &#91;were they&#93; youri merchants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> The merchants of Sheva &#91;Seven&#93; and Ra’mah &#91;horse’s mane&#93;, they &#91;were&#93; youri merchants: they occupied in youri wares (something gotten at the fair) with chief of all spices, and with all precious stones, and gold.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Haran &#91;mountaineer&#93;, and Kaneh &#91;to give a flattering title&#93;, and ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93;, the merchants of Sheva &#91;Seven&#93;, Ashur &#91;A Level Plain&#93;, &#91;and&#93; Kilmad &#91;enclosure&#93;, &#91;were&#93; youri merchants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> These &#91;were&#93; youri merchants in all sorts &#91;of things&#93;, in blue clothes, and adorned with needlework work, and in chests of rich apparel, bound with cords, and made of cedar, among youri merchandise.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> The ships of Tarshish &#91;yellow jasper&#93; did sing of youi in youri market: and youi were replenished, and made very glorious in the midst of the seas.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> Your1s rowers have brought youi into great waters: the east wind has broken youi in the midst of the seas.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Your1s riches, and youri wares (something gotten at the fair), youri merchandise, youri mariners, and youri pilots, youri caulkers, and the occupiers of youri merchandise, and all youri men of war, that &#91;are&#93; in youi, and in all youri company which &#91;is&#93; in the midst of youi, shall fall into the midst of the seas in the day of youri ruin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> The suburbs shall shake at the sound of the cry of youri pilots.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And all that handle the oar, the mariners, &#91;and&#93; all the pilots of the sea, shall come down from their ships, they shall stand upon the land;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And shall cause their voice to be heard against youi, and shall cry bitterly, and shall cast up dust upon their heads, they shall wallow (to approve, commend, accept) themselves in the ashes:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And they shall make themselves utterly bald for youi, and gird them with sackcloth, and they shall weep for youi with bitterness of heart &#91;and&#93; bitter wailing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And in their wailing they shall take up a lamentation for youi, and lament over youi, &#91;saying&#93;, What &#91;city is&#93; like Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, like the destroyed in the midst of the sea?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> When youri wares went forth out of the seas, youi filled many people; youi did enrich the kings of the earth with the multitude of youri riches and of youri merchandise.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> In the time &#91;when&#93; youi shall be broken by the seas in the depths of the waters youri merchandise and all youri company in the midst of youi shall fall.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> All the inhabitants of the isles shall be astonished at youi, and their kings shall be sore afraid, they shall be troubled in &#91;their&#93; Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> The merchants among the people shall hiss at youi; youi shall be a terror, and never &#91;shall be&#93; any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came again unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, say unto the prince of Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youri heart &#91;is&#93; lifted up, and youi have said, I &#91;am&#93; a God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, I sit &#91;in&#93; the seat of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, in the midst of the seas; yet youi &#91;are&#93; a man, and not God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, though youi set youri heart as the heart of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Behold, youi &#91;are&#93; wiser than Dani’el &#91;God is Judge&#93;; there is no secret that they can hide from youi:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> With youri wisdom and with youri understanding youi have gotten youi riches, and have gotten gold and silver into youri treasures:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> By youri great wisdom &#91;and&#93; by youri traffick (trade; do business) have youi increased youri riches, and youri heart is lifted up because of youri riches:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youi have set youri heart as the heart of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Behold, therefore I will bring strangers upon youi, the terrible of the nations: and they shall draw their swords against the beauty of youri wisdom, and they shall defile youri brightness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> They shall bring youi down to the pit, and youi shall die the deaths of &#91;them that are&#93; slain in the midst of the seas.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Will youi yet say before him that slays youi, I &#91;am&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;? but youi &#91;shall be&#93; a man, and no God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, in the hand of him that slays youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> youi shall die the deaths of the uncircumcised by the hand of strangers: for I have spoken &#91;it&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Son of man, take up a lamentation upon the king of Tzor (Tyrus) &#91;a rock&#93;, and say unto him, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); youi seal up the sum, full of wisdom, and perfect in beauty.</span>

<div className="bp">
