<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> And I will scatter the Mitzri among the nations, and disperse them among the countries; and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And it came to pass in the eleventh year, in the third &#91;month&#93;, in the first &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, speak unto Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and to his multitude; Whom are youi like in youi greatness?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Behold, the Ashur (Assyrian) &#91;was&#93; a cedar in L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93; with fair branches, and with a shadowing shroud, and of an high stature; and his top was among the thick boughs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> The waters made him great, the deep set him up on high with her rivers running round about his plants, and sent out her little rivers unto all the trees of the field.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Therefore his height was exalted above all the trees of the field, and his boughs were multiplied, and his branches became long because of the multitude of waters, when he shot forth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> All the fowls of heaven made their nests in his boughs, and under his branches did all the beasts of the field bring forth their young, and under his shadow dwelt all great nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Thus was he fair in his greatness, in the length of his branches: for his root was by great waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> The cedars in the garden of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; could not hide him: the fir trees were not like his boughs, and the chestnut trees were not like his branches; nor any tree in the garden of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; was like unto him in his beauty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> I have made him fair by the multitude of his branches: so that all the trees of ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93;, that &#91;were&#93; in the garden of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, envied him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because youi have lifted up yourself in height, and he has shot up his top among the thick boughs, and his heart is lifted up in his height;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> I have therefore delivered him into the hand of the mighty one of the heathen; he shall surely deal with him: I have driven him out for his wickedness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And strangers, the terrible of the nations, have cut him off, and have left him: upon the mountains and in all the valleys his branches are fallen, and his boughs are broken by all the rivers of the land; and all the people of the earth are gone down from his shadow, and have left him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Upon his ruin shall all the fowls of the heaven remain, and all the beasts of the field shall be upon his branches:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> To the end that none of all the trees by the waters exalt themselves for their height, neither shoot up their top among the thick boughs, neither their trees stand up in their height, all that drink water: for they are all delivered unto death, to the lower parts of the earth, in the midst of the children of men, with them that go down to the pit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); In the day when he went down to the grave I caused a mourning: I covered the deep for him, and I restrained the floods thereof, and the great waters were detained (held): and I caused L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93; to mourn for him, and all the trees of the field fainted for him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> I made the nations to shake at the sound of his fall, when I cast him down to hell with them that descend into the pit: and all the trees of ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93;, the choice and best of L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, all that drink water, shall be comforted in the lower parts of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> They also went down into hell with him unto &#91;them that be&#93; slain with the sword; and &#91;they that were&#93; his arm, &#91;that&#93; dwelt under his shadow in the midst of the heathen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> To whom are youi thus like in glory and in greatness among the trees of ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93;? yet shall youi be brought down with the trees of ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93; unto the lower parts of the earth: youi shall lie in the midst of the uncircumcised with &#91;them that be&#93; slain by the sword. This &#91;is&#93; Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; and all his multitude, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And it came to pass in the twelfth year, in the twelfth month, in the first &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, take up a lamentation for Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and say unto him, youi are like a young lion of the nations, and youi &#91;are&#93; as a whale in the seas: and youi came forth with youri rivers, and troubled the waters with youri feet, and fouled their rivers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will therefore spread out my net over youi with a company of many people; and they shall bring youi up in my net.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Then will I leave youi upon the land, I will cast youi forth upon the open field, and will cause all the fowls of the heaven to remain upon youi, and I will fill the beasts of the whole earth with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> And I will lay youri flesh upon the mountains, and fill the valleys with youri height.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> I will also water with youri blood the land wherein youi swim, &#91;even&#93; to the mountains; and the rivers shall be full of youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And when I shall put youi out, I will cover the heaven, and make the stars thereof dark; I will cover the sun with a cloud, and the moon shall not give her light.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> All the bright lights of heaven will I make dark over youi, and set darkness upon youri land, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> I will also vex the hearts of many people, when I shall bring youri destruction among the nations, into the countries which youi have not known.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Yes, I will make many people amazed at youi, and their kings shall be horribly afraid for youi, when I shall brandish my sword before them; and they shall tremble at &#91;every&#93; moment, every man for his own life, in the day of youri fall.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); The sword of the king of Bavel (to jumble, or a confused medley of sounds; confusion; gate of the deity) (Babylon) &#91;confusion&#93; shall come upon youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> By the swords of the mighty will I cause youri multitude to fall, the terrible of the nations, all of them: and they shall spoil (plunder; prey; booty) the pomp of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and all the multitude thereof shall be destroyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> I will destroy also all the beasts thereof from beside the great waters; neither shall the foot of man trouble them anymore, nor the hoofs of beasts trouble them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Then will I make their waters deep, and cause their rivers to run like oil, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> When I shall make the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; desolate, and the country shall be destitute of that whereof it was full, when I shall strike all them that dwell in it, then shall they know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> This &#91;is&#93; the lamentation which they shall lament her: the daughters of the nations shall lament her: they shall lament for her, &#91;even&#93; for Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and for all her multitude, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> It came to pass also in the twelfth year, in the fifteenth &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> Son of man, wail for the multitude of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and cast them down, &#91;even&#93; her, and the daughters of the famous nations, unto the lower parts of the earth, with them that go down into the pit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> Whom do youi pass in beauty? go down, and be youi laid with the uncircumcised.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> They shall fall in the midst of &#91;them that are&#93; slain by the sword: she is delivered to the sword: draw her and all her multitudes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> The strong among the mighty shall speak to him out of the midst of hell with them that help him: they are gone down, they lie uncircumcised, slain by the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Ashur &#91;A Level Plain&#93; &#91;is&#93; there and all her company: his graves &#91;are&#93; about him: all of them slain, fallen by the sword:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Whose graves are set in the sides of the pit, and her company is round about her grave: all of them slain, fallen by the sword, which caused terror in the land of the living.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> There &#91;is&#93; ‘Eilam &#91;eternity&#93; and all her multitude round about her grave, all of them slain, fallen by the sword, which are gone down uncircumcised into the lower parts of the earth, which caused their terror in the land of the living; yet have they borne their shame with them that go down to the pit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> They have set her a bed in the midst of the slain with all her multitude: her graves &#91;are&#93; round about him: all of them uncircumcised, slain by the sword: though their terror was caused in the land of the living, yet have they borne their shame with them that go down to the pit: he is put in the midst of &#91;them that be&#93; slain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> There &#91;is&#93; Meshekh &#91;drawing out&#93;, Tuval &#91;you shall be brought&#93;, and all her multitude: her graves &#91;are&#93; round about him: all of them uncircumcised, slain by the sword, though they caused their terror in the land of the living.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> And they shall not lie with the mighty &#91;that are&#93; fallen of the uncircumcised, which are gone down to hell with their weapons of war: and they have laid their swords under their heads, but their iniquities shall be upon their bones, though &#91;they were&#93; the terror of the mighty in the land of the living.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> Yes, youi shall be broken in the midst of the uncircumcised, and shall lie with &#91;them that are&#93; slain with the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> There &#91;is&#93; Edom &#91;Red&#93;, her kings, and all her princes, which with their might are laid by &#91;them that were&#93; slain by the sword: they shall lie with the uncircumcised, and with them that go down to the pit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> There &#91;be&#93; the princes of the north, all of them, and all the people of Tzidon &#91;Hunting&#93;, which are gone down with the slain; with their terror they are ashamed of their might; and they lie uncircumcised with &#91;them that be&#93; slain by the sword, and bear their shame with them that go down to the pit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; shall see them, and shall be comforted over all his multitude, &#91;even&#93; Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; and all his army slain by the sword, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> For I have caused my terror in the land of the living: and he shall be laid in the midst of the uncircumcised with &#91;them that are&#93; slain with the sword, &#91;even&#93; Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; and all his multitude, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Again the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, speak to the children of youri people, and say unto them, When I bring the sword upon a land, if the people of the land take a man of their coasts, and set him for their watchman:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> If when he sees the sword come upon the land, he blow the trumpet, and warn the people;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Then whosoever hears the sound of the trumpet, and takes not warning; if the sword come, and take him away, his blood shall be upon his own head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> He heard the sound of the trumpet, and took not warning; his blood shall be upon him. But he that takes warning shall deliver his soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> But if the watchman see the sword come, and blow not the trumpet, and the people be not warned; if the sword come, and take &#91;any&#93; person from among them, he is taken away in his iniquity; but his blood will I require at the watchman’s hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> So youi, O Son of man, I have set youi a watchman unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; therefore youi shall hear the word at my mouth, and warn them from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> When I say unto the wicked, O wicked &#91;man&#93;, youi shall surely die; if youi do not speak to warn the wicked from his way, that wicked &#91;man&#93; shall die in his iniquity; but his blood will I require at youri hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Nevertheless, if youi warn the wicked of his way to turn from it; if he do not turn from his way, he shall die in his iniquity; but youi have delivered youri soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Therefore, O youi Son of man, speak unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; Thus you2f speak, saying, If our transgressions and our sins &#91;be&#93; upon us, and we pine away in them, how should we then live?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Say unto them, &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), I have no pleasure in the death of the wicked; but that the wicked turn from his way and live: turn you2f, turn you2f from your evil ways; for why will you2f die, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Therefore, youi Son of man, say unto the children of youri people, The righteousness of the righteous shall not deliver him in the day of his transgression: as for the wickedness of the wicked, he shall not fall thereby in the day that he turns from his wickedness; neither shall the righteous be able to live for his &#91;righteousness&#93; in the day that he sins.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> When I shall say to the righteous, &#91;that&#93; he shall surely live; if he trust to his own righteousness, and commit iniquity, all his righteousnesses shall not be remembered; but for his iniquity that he has committed, he shall die for it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Again, when I say unto the wicked, youi shall surely die; if he turn from his sin, and do that which is lawful and right;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> &#91;If&#93; the wicked restore the pledge, give again that he had robbed, walk in the statutes of life, without committing iniquity; he shall surely live, he shall not die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> None of his sins that he has committed shall be mentioned unto him: he has done that which is lawful and right; he shall surely live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Yet the children of youi people say, The way of the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; is not equal: but as for them, their way is not equal.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> When the righteous turns from his righteousness, and commit iniquity, he shall even die thereby.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> But if the wicked turn from his wickedness, and do that which is lawful and right, he shall live thereby.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> Yet you2f say, The way of the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; is not equal. O you2f house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, I will judge you everyone after his ways.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And it came to pass in the twelfth year of our captivity, in the tenth &#91;month&#93;, in the fifth &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; one that had escaped out of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; came unto me, saying, The city is smitten.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Now the hand of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was upon me in the evening, afore he that was escaped came; and had opened my mouth, until he came to me in the morning; and my mouth was opened, and I was no more dumb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Then the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> Son of man, they that inhabit those wastes of the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; speak, saying, Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93; was one, and he inherited the land: but we &#91;are&#93; many; the land is given us for inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> For what reason say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); You2f eat with the blood, and lift up your eyes toward your idols, and shed blood: and shall you2f possess the land?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> You2f stand upon your sword, you2f work abomination, and you2f defile everyone his neighbour’s wife: and shall you2f possess the land?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> Say youi thus unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); &#91;As&#93; I live, surely they that &#91;are&#93; in the wastes shall fall by the sword, and him that &#91;is&#93; in the open field will I give to the beasts to be devoured, and they that &#91;be&#93; in the forts and in the caves shall die of the pestilence.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> For I will lay the land most desolate, and the pomp of her strength shall cease; and the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall be desolate, that none shall pass through.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Then shall they know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I have laid the land most desolate because of all their abominations which they have committed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Also, youi Son of man, the children of youi people still are talking against youi by the walls and in the doors of the houses, and speak one to another, everyone to his brother, saying, Come, I pray you, and hear what is the word that comes forth from the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And they come unto youi as the people comes, and they sit before youi &#91;as&#93; my people, and they hear youri words, but they will not do them: for with their mouth they show much love, &#91;but&#93; their heart goes after their covetousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And, lo, youi &#91;are&#93; unto them as a very lovely song of one that has a pleasant voice, and can play well on an instrument: for they hear youri words, but they do them not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And when this comes to pass, (lo, it will come,) then shall they know that a prophet has been among them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, prophesy against the shepherds of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, prophesy, and say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) unto the shepherds; Woe &#91;be&#93; to the shepherds of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that do feed themselves! should not the shepherds feed the flocks?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> You2f eat the fat, and you2f clothe you with the wool, you2f kill them that are fed: &#91;but&#93; you2f feed not the flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> The diseased have you2f not strengthened, neither have you2f healed that which was sick, neither have you2f bound up &#91;that which was&#93; broken, neither have you2f brought again that which was driven away, neither have you2f sought that which was lost; but with force and with cruelty have you2f ruled them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> And they were scattered, because &#91;there is&#93; no shepherd: and they became meat to all the beasts of the field, when they were scattered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> My sheep wandered through all the mountains, and upon every high hill: yes, my flock was scattered upon all the face of the earth, and none did search or seek &#91;after them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Therefore, you2f shepherds, hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> &#91;As&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), surely because my flock became a prey, and my flock became meat to every beast of the field, because &#91;there was&#93; no shepherd, neither did my shepherds search for my flock, but the shepherds fed themselves, and fed not my flock;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Therefore, O you2f shepherds, hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate);</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against the shepherds; and I will require my flock at their hand, and cause them to cease from feeding the flock; neither shall the shepherds feed themselves anymore; for I will deliver my flock from their mouth, that they may not be meat for them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> For thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I, &#91;even&#93; I, will both search my sheep, and seek them out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> As a shepherd seeks out his flock in the day that he is among his sheep &#91;that are&#93; scattered; so will I seek out my sheep, and will deliver them out of all places where they have been scattered in the cloudy and dark day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> And I will bring them out from the people, and gather them from the countries, and will bring them to their own land, and feed them upon the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; by the rivers, and in all the inhabited places of the country.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> I will feed them in a good pasture, and upon the high mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall their fold be: there shall they lie in a good fold, and &#91;in&#93; a fat pasture shall they feed upon the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> I will feed my flock, and I will cause them to lie down, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> I will seek that which was lost, and bring again that which was driven away, and will bind up &#91;that which was&#93; broken, and will strengthen that which was sick: but I will destroy the fat and the strong; I will feed them with judgment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> And &#91;as for&#93; you, O my flock, thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I judge between cattle and cattle, between the rams and the he goats.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> &#91;Seem it&#93; a small thing unto you to have eaten up the good pasture, but you2f must trample down with your feet the residue (part that remains, the rest) of your pastures? and to have drunk of the deep waters, but you2f must foul the residue (part that remains, the rest) with your feet?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And &#91;as for&#93; my flock, they eat that which you2f have trampled with your feet; and they drink that which you2f have fouled with your feet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) unto them; Behold, I, &#91;even&#93; I, will judge between the fat cattle and between the lean cattle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> Because you2f have thrust with side and with shoulder, and pushed all the diseased with your horns, till you2f have scattered them abroad;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Therefore will I save my flock, and they shall no more be a prey; and I will judge between cattle and cattle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> And I will set up one shepherd over them, and he shall feed them, &#91;even&#93; my servant David &#91;beloved&#93;; he shall feed them, and he shall be their shepherd.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> And I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and my servant David &#91;beloved&#93; a prince among them; I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> And I will make with them a covenant of peace, and will cause the evil beasts to cease out of the land: and they shall dwell safely in the wilderness, and sleep in the woods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> And I will make them and the places round about my hill a blessing; and I will cause the shower to come down in his season; there shall be showers of blessing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> And the tree of the field shall yield her fruit, and the earth shall yield her increase, and they shall be safe in their land, and shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I have broken the bands of their yoke, and delivered them out of the hand of those that served themselves of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> And they shall no more be a prey to the heathen, neither shall the beast of the land devour them; but they shall dwell safely, and none shall make &#91;them&#93; afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And I will raise up for them a plant of renown (great reputation; being well-known), and they shall be no more consumed with hunger in the land, neither bear the shame of the heathen any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Thus shall they know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;am&#93; with them, and &#91;that&#93; they, &#91;even&#93; the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;are&#93; my people, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And you2f my flock, the flock of my pasture, &#91;are&#93; men, &#91;and&#93; I &#91;am&#93; your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, set youri face against mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, and prophesy against it,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say unto it, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, O mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, I &#91;am&#93; against youi, and I will stretch out my hand against youi, and I will make youi most desolate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> I will lay youri cities waste, and youi shall be desolate, and youi shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Because youi have had a perpetual hatred, and have shed &#91;the blood of&#93; the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; by the force of the sword in the time of their calamity, in the time &#91;that their&#93; iniquity &#91;had&#93; an end:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Therefore, &#91;as&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), I will prepare youi unto blood, and blood shall pursue youi: since youi have not hated blood, even blood shall pursue youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Thus will I make mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93; most desolate, and cut off from it him that passes out and him that returns.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And I will fill his mountains with his slain &#91;men&#93;: in youi hills, and in youi valleys, and in all youri rivers, shall they fall that are slain with the sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> I will make youi perpetual desolations, and youri cities shall not return: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Because youi have said, These two nations and these two countries shall be mine, and we will possess it; whereas the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was there:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Therefore, &#91;as&#93; I live, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), I will even do according to yourri anger, and according to youi envy which youi have used out of youri hatred against them; and I will make myself known among them, when I have judged youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> And youi shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;and that&#93; I have heard all youri blasphemies which youi have spoken against the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, They are laid desolate, they are given us to consume.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Thus with your mouth you2f have boasted against me, and have multiplied your words against Me: I have heard &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); When the whole earth rejoices, I will make youi desolate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> As youi did rejoice at the inheritance of the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, because it was desolate, so will I do unto youi: youi shall be desolate, O mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, and all Edom &#91;red&#93;, &#91;even&#93; all of it: and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> Also, youi Son of man, prophesy unto the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say, You2f mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because the enemy has said against you, Aha, even the ancient high places are ours in possession:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> Therefore prophesy and say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because they have made &#91;you&#93; desolate, and swallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed you up on every side, that you2f might be a possession unto the residue (part that remains, the rest) of the heathen, and you2f are taken up in the lips of talkers, and &#91;are&#93; an infamy of the people:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Therefore, you2f mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, hear the word of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) to the mountains, and to the hills, to the rivers, and to the valleys, to the desolate wastes, and to the cities that are forsaken, which became a prey and ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats) to the residue (part that remains, the rest) of the heathen that &#91;are&#93; round about;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Surely in the fire of my jealousy have I spoken against the residue (part that remains, the rest) of the heathen, and against all Edom &#91;red&#93;, which have appointed my land into their possession with the joy of all &#91;their&#93; heart, with despiteful minds, to cast it out for a prey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Prophesy therefore concerning the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and say unto the mountains, and to the hills, to the rivers, and to the valleys, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I have spoken in my jealousy and in my fury, because you2f have borne the shame of the heathen:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I have lifted up my hand, Surely the heathen that &#91;are&#93; about you, they shall bear their shame.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> But you2f, O mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, you2f shall shoot forth your branches, and yield your fruit to my people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; for they are at hand to come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> For, behold, I &#91;am&#93; for you, and I will turn unto you, and you2f shall be tilled and sown:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> And I will multiply men upon you, all the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;even&#93; all of it: and the cities shall be inhabited, and the wastes shall be built:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And I will multiply upon you man and beast; and they shall increase and bring fruit: and I will settle you after your old estates, and will do better &#91;unto you&#93; than at your beginnings: and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Yes, I will cause men to walk upon you, &#91;even&#93; my people Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and they shall possess youi, and youi shall be their inheritance, and youi shall no more from this time forth (from now on) bereave them &#91;of men&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Because they say unto you, youri &#91;land&#93; devours up men, and have bereaved youri nations;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> Therefore youi shall devour men no more, neither bereave youri nations any more, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> Neither will I cause &#91;men&#93; to hear in youi the shame of the heathen any more, neither shall youi bear the reproach (disgrace; shame) of the people any more, neither shall youi cause youri nations to fall any more, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Moreover the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Son of man, when the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; dwelt in their own land, they defiled it by their own way and by their doings: their way was before me as the uncleanness of a removed woman.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> For what reason I poured my fury upon them for the blood that they had shed upon the land, and for their idols &#91;which&#93; they had polluted it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And I scattered them among the heathen, and they were dispersed through the countries: according to their way and according to their doings I judged them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> And when they entered unto the heathen, to which they went, they profaned my holy name, when they said to them, These &#91;are&#93; the people of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and are gone forth out of his land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> But I had pity for my holy name, which the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; had profaned among the heathen, to which they went.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Therefore say unto the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I do not &#91;this&#93; for your sakes, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, but for my holy name’s sake, which you2f have profaned among the heathen, to which you2f went.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> And I will sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) my great name, which was profaned among the heathen, which you2f have profaned in the midst of them; and the heathen shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), when I shall be sanctified in you before their eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> For I will take you from among the heathen, and gather you out of all countries, and will bring you into your own land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Then will I sprinkle clean water upon you, and you2f shall be clean: from all your filthiness, and from all your idols, will I cleanse you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> A new heart also will I give you, and a new spirit will I put within you: and I will take away the stony heart out of your flesh, and I will give you an heart of flesh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> And I will put my Spirit within you, and cause you to walk in my statutes, and you2f shall keep my judgments, and do &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> And you2f shall dwell in the land that I gave to your fathers; and you2f shall be my people, and I will be your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> I will also save you from all your uncleannesses: and I will call for the corn, and will increase it, and lay no famine upon you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And I will multiply the fruit of the tree, and the increase of the field, that you2f shall receive no more reproach (disgrace; shame) of famine among the heathen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Then shall you2f remember your own evil ways, and your doings that &#91;were&#93; not good, and shall loathe yourselves in your own sight for your iniquities and for your abominations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Not for your sakes do I &#91;this&#93;, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), be it known unto you: be ashamed and confounded for your own ways, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); In the day that I shall have cleansed you from all your iniquities I will also cause &#91;you&#93; to dwell in the cities, and the wastes shall be built.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And the desolate land shall be tilled, whereas it lay desolate in the sight of all that passed by.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And they shall say, This land that was desolate is become like the garden of ‘Eden &#91;pleasure&#93;; and the waste and desolate and ruined cities &#91;are become&#93; fenced, &#91;and&#93; are inhabited.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Then the heathen that are left round about you shall know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) build the ruined &#91;places, and&#93; plant that that was desolate: I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;, and I will do &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); I will yet &#91;for&#93; this be enquired of by the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, to do &#91;it&#93; for them; I will increase them with men like a flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> As the holy flock, as the flock of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation of Peace&#93; in her solemn feasts; so shall the waste cities be filled with flocks of men: and they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> The hand of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was upon me, and carried me out in the Spirit of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and set me down in the midst of the valley which &#91;was&#93; full of bones,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> And caused me to pass by them round about: and, behold, &#91;there were&#93; very many in the open valley; and, lo, &#91;they were&#93; very dry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And he said unto me, Son of man, can these bones live? And I answered, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER), youi know.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> Again he said unto me, Prophesy upon these bones, and say unto them, O you2f dry bones, hear the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER) unto these bones; Behold, I will cause breath to enter into you, and you2f shall live:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> And I will lay sinews upon you, and will bring up flesh upon you, and cover you with skin, and put breath in you, and you2f shall live; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> So I prophesied as I was commanded: and as I prophesied, there was a noise, and behold a shaking, and the bones came together, bone to his bone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> And when I beheld, lo, the sinews and the flesh came up upon them, and the skin covered them above: but &#91;there was&#93; no breath in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Then said he unto me, Prophesy unto the wind, prophesy, Son of man, and say to the wind, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Come from the four winds, O breath, and breathe upon these slain, that they may live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> So I prophesied as he commanded me, and the breath came into them, and they lived, and stood up upon their feet, an exceeding great army.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> Then he said unto me, Son of man, these bones are the whole house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: behold, they say, Our bones are dried, and our hope is lost: we are cut off for our parts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> Therefore prophesy and say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, O my people, I will open your graves, and cause you to come up out of your graves, and bring you into the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> And you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when I have opened your graves, O my people, and brought you up out of your graves,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And shall put my spirit in you, and you2f shall live, and I shall place you in your own land: then shall you2f know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have spoken &#91;it&#93;, and performed &#91;it&#93;, says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> The word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came again unto me, saying,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> Moreover, youi Son of man, take youi one stick, and write upon it, For Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and for the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; his companions: then take another stick, and write upon it, For Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, the stick of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and &#91;for&#93; all the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; his companions:</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> And join them one to another into one stick; and they shall become one in youi hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> And when the children of youri people shall speak unto youi, saying, Will youi not show us what youi &#91;mean&#93; by these?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> Say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will take the stick of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; in the hand of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; his fellows, and will put them with him, &#91;even&#93; with the stick of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, and make them one stick, and they shall be one in my hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> And the sticks whereon youi write shall be in youri hand before their eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And say unto them, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I will take the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; from among the heathen, to which they be gone, and will gather them on every side, and bring them into their own land:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> And I will make them one nation in the land upon the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and one king shall be king to them all: and they shall be no more two nations, neither shall they be divided into two kingdoms any more at all:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> Neither shall they defile themselves any more with their idols, nor with their detestable things, nor with any of their transgressions: but I will save them out of all their dwellingplaces, wherein they have sinned, and will cleanse them: so shall they be my people, and I will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> And David &#91;beloved&#93; my servant &#91;shall be&#93; king over them; and they all shall have one shepherd: they shall also walk in my judgments, and observe my statutes, and do them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> And they shall dwell in the land that I have given unto Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; my servant, wherein your fathers have dwelt; and they shall dwell in it, &#91;even&#93; they, and their children, and their children’s children forever: and my servant David &#91;beloved&#93; &#91;shall be&#93; their prince forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> Moreover I will make a covenant of peace with them; it shall be an everlasting covenant with them: and I will place them, and multiply them, and will set my sanctuary in the midst of them forevermore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> My tabernacle also shall be with them: yes, I will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and they shall be my people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> And the heathen shall know that I the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) do sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, when my sanctuary shall be in the midst of them forevermore.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> And the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) came unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> Son of man, set youri face against Gog &#91;mountain&#93;, the land of Magog &#91;land of God&#93;, the chief prince of Meshekh &#91;drawing out&#93; and Tuval &#91;you shall be brought&#93;, and prophesy against him,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Ezekiel 3:14; Ezekiel 3:22; 2 Kings 3:15"> And say, Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Behold, I &#91;am&#93; against youi, O Gog &#91;mountain&#93;, the chief prince of Meshekh &#91;drawing out&#93; and Tuval &#91;you shall be brought&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> And I will turn youi back, and put hooks into youri jaws, and I will bring youi forth, and all youri army, horses and horsemen, all of them clothed with all sorts &#91;of armour, even&#93; a great company &#91;with&#93; bucklers and shields, all of them handling swords:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> Paras (Persia) &#91;pure or splendid&#93;, Ethiopia &#91;Kush&#93;, and Libya with them; all of them with shield and helmet:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7–8; Ezekiel 1:15"> Gomer &#91;complete&#93;, and all his bands; the house of Togarmah &#91;you will break her&#93; of the north quarters, and all his bands: &#91;and&#93; many people with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> Be youi prepared, and prepare for yourself, youi, and all youri company that are assembled unto youi, and be youi a guard unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> After many days youi shall be visited: in the latter years youi shall come into the land &#91;that is&#93; brought back from the sword, &#91;and is&#93; gathered out of many people, against the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which have been always waste: but it is brought forth out of the nations, and they shall dwell safely all of them.</span>

<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> Thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); It shall also come to pass, &#91;that&#93; at the same time shall things come into youri mind, and youi shall think an evil thought:</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> So that the fishes of the sea, and the fowls of the heaven, and the beasts of the field, and all creeping things that creep upon the earth, and all the men that &#91;are&#93; upon the face of the earth, shall shake at my presence, and the mountains shall be thrown down, and the steep places shall fall, and every wall shall fall to (to do entirely, do wholly) the ground.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> And the travelers &#91;that&#93; pass through the land, when &#91;any&#93; sees a man’s bone, then shall he set up a sign by it, till the buriers have buried it in the valley of Hamon-Gog &#91;multitude of Gog&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> And, youi Son of man, thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Speak unto every feathered fowl, and to every beast of the field, Assemble yourselves, and come; gather yourselves on every side to my sacrifice that I do sacrifice for you, &#91;even&#93; a great sacrifice upon the mountains of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that you2f may eat flesh, and drink blood.</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> Therefore thus says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER); Now will I bring again the captivity of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, and have mercy upon the whole house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and will be jealous for my holy name;</span>

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> Then shall they know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, which caused them to be led into captivity among the heathen: but I have gathered them unto their own land, and have left none of them anymore there.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Gosel 3:16; Ezekiel 11:24; Acts 7:56"> In the five and twentieth year of our captivity, in the beginning of the year, in the tenth &#91;day&#93; of the month, in the fourteenth year after that the city was smitten, in the selfsame day the hand of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was upon me, and brought me there.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="2 Kings 24:12–15; Ezekiel 8:1; Ezekiel 20:1"> In the visions of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; brought he me into the land of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and set me upon a very high mountain, by which &#91;was&#93; as the frame of a city on the south.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Jeremiah 23:19"> And the man said unto me, Son of man, behold with youri eyes, and hear with youri ears, and set youri heart upon all that I shall show youi; for to the intent that I might show &#91;them&#93; unto youi &#91;are&#93; youi brought here: declare all that youi see to the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Revelation 4:6–8; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 6:6"> And behold a wall on the outside of the house round about, and in the man’s hand a measuring reed of six cubits &#91;long&#93; by the cubit and an hand breadth: so he measured the breadth of the building, one reed; and the height, one reed.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Revelation 1:15; Daniel 10:6; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4"> And &#91;every&#93; little chamber &#91;was&#93; one reed long, and one reed broad; and between the little chambers &#91;were&#93; five cubits; and the threshold of the gate by the porch of the gate within &#91;was&#93; one reed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:21; Ezekiel 10:11; Isaiah 6:6"> He measured also the porch of the gate within, one reed.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:22; Ezekiel 10:11; Ezekiel 1:11–12"> Then measured he the porch of the gate, eight cubits; and the posts thereof, two cubits; and the porch of the gate &#91;was&#93; inward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:14; Revelation 4:7; 1 Corinthians 9:9–10"> And the little chambers of the gate eastward &#91;were&#93; three on this side, and three on that side; they three &#91;were&#93; of one measure: and the posts had one measure on this side and on that side.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Isaiah 6:2; Ezekiel 1:23; Ezekiel 10:19"> And he measured the breadth of the entry of the gate, ten cubits; &#91;and&#93; the length of the gate, thirteen cubits.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 10:22; Hebrews 1:14"> The space also before the little chambers &#91;was&#93; one cubit &#91;on this side&#93;, and the space &#91;was&#93; one cubit on that side: and the little chambers &#91;were&#93; six cubits on this side, and six cubits on that side.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> He measured then the gate from the roof of &#91;one&#93; little chamber to the roof of another: the breadth &#91;was&#93; five and twenty cubits, door against door.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> And from the face of the gate of the entrance unto the face of the porch of the inner gate &#91;were&#93; fifty cubits.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> And &#91;there were&#93; narrow windows to the little chambers, and to their posts within the gate round about, and likewise to the arches: and windows &#91;were&#93; round about inward: and upon &#91;each&#93; post &#91;were&#93; palm trees.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> Then brought he me into the outward court, and, lo, &#91;there were&#93; chambers, and a pavement made for the court round about: thirty chambers &#91;were&#93; upon the pavement.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> And the gate of the outward court that looked toward the north, he measured the length thereof, and the breadth thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And the little chambers thereof &#91;were&#93; three on this side and three on that side; and the posts thereof and the arches thereof were after the measure of the first gate: the length thereof &#91;was&#93; fifty cubits, and the breadth five and twenty cubits.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> And their windows, and their arches, and their palm trees, &#91;were&#93; after the measure of the gate that looks toward the east; and they went up unto it by seven steps; and the arches thereof &#91;were&#93; before them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> And &#91;there were&#93; windows in it and in the arches thereof round about, like those windows: the length &#91;was&#93; fifty cubits, and the breadth five and twenty cubits.</span>
