</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Revelation 4:5; Tehillim - Psalms 104:4; Gosel 28:3"> And over against the border of the priests the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93; &#91;shall have&#93; five and twenty thousand in length, and ten thousand in breadth: all the length &#91;shall be&#93; five and twenty thousand, and the breadth ten thousand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Gosel 24:27; Zechariah 4:10; Mark 13:27"> And they shall not sell of it, neither exchange, nor alienate the firstfruits of the land: for &#91;it is&#93; holy unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Daniel 7:9; Ezekiel 10:9; Ezekiel 1:19–21"> And the five thousand, that are left in the breadth over against the five and twenty thousand, shall be a Profane (treat as common; defile) &#91;place&#93; for the city, for dwelling, and for suburbs: and the city shall be in the midst thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Daniel 10:6; Ezekiel 10:9–11; Exodus 39:13"> And these &#91;shall be&#93; the measures thereof; the north side four thousand and five hundred, and the south side four thousand and five hundred, and on the east side four thousand and five hundred, and the west side four thousand and five hundred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:9; Ezekiel 1:12; Isaiah 55:11"> And the suburbs of the city shall be toward the north two hundred and fifty, and toward the south two hundred and fifty, and toward the east two hundred and fifty, and toward the west two hundred and fifty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:12; Revelation 4:6; Revelation 4:8"> And the residue (part that remains, the rest) in length over against the oblation of the holy &#91;portion shall be&#93; ten thousand eastward, and ten thousand westward: and it shall be over against the oblation of the holy &#91;portion&#93;; and the increase thereof shall be for food unto them that serve the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:16; Ezekiel 10:19; Tehillim - Psalms 103:20"> And they that serve the city shall serve it out of all the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:12; Ezekiel 10:17; 1 Corinthians 14:32"> All the oblation &#91;shall be&#93; five and twenty thousand by five and twenty thousand: you2f shall offer the holy oblation Foursquare (squared; four cornered), with the possession of the city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:17; Ezekiel 1:19–20; Romans 8:2"> And the residue (part that remains, the rest) &#91;shall be&#93; for the prince, on the one side and on the other of the holy oblation, and of the possession of the city, over against the five and twenty thousand of the oblation toward the east border, and westward over against the five and twenty thousand toward the west border, over against the portions for the prince: and it shall be the holy oblation; and the sanctuary of the house &#91;shall be&#93; in the midst thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Ezekiel 1:26; Revelation 4:6"> Moreover from the possession of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, and from the possession of the city, &#91;being&#93; in the midst &#91;of that&#93; which is the prince’s, between the border of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; and the border of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, shall be for the prince.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Job 4:18; Ezekiel 1:11–12; Tehillim - Psalms 89:7"> As for the rest of the tribes, from the east side unto the west side, Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93; &#91;shall have&#93; a &#91;portion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Ezekiel 43:2; Daniel 10:6; Revelation 1:15"> And by the border of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, from the east side unto the west side, Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93; &#91;shall have&#93; a &#91;portion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:22"> And by the border of Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, from the east side unto the west side, Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93; a &#91;portion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Ezekiel 10:1; Exodus 24:10; Revelation 1:13"> And by the border of Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, from the east side unto the west side, Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93; a &#91;portion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Ezekiel 1:4; Ezekiel 8:2; Hebrews 12:29"> And by the border of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, from the east side unto the west side, Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; a &#91;portion&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Revelation 10:1; Revelation 4:3; Genesis 17:3"> And by the border of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, at the south side southward, the border shall be even from Tamar &#91;palm-tree&#93; &#91;unto&#93; the waters of strife &#91;in&#93; Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93;, &#91;and&#93; to the river toward the great sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> This &#91;is&#93; the land which you2f shall divide by lot unto the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; for inheritance, and these &#91;are&#93; their portions, says the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih (THE FATHER).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And these &#91;are&#93; the goings out of the city on the north side, four thousand and five hundred measures.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And the gates of the city &#91;shall be&#93; after the names of the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: three gates northward; one gate of Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, one gate of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, one gate of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And at the east side four thousand and five hundred: and three gates; and one gate of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, one gate of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;, one gate of Dan &#91;Judge&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And at the south side four thousand and five hundred measures: and three gates; one gate of Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, one gate of Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, one gate of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> At the west side four thousand and five hundred, &#91;with&#93; their three gates; one gate of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, one gate of Asher &#91;Happy&#93;, one gate of Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> &#91;It was&#93; round about eighteen thousand &#91;measures&#93;: and the name of the city from &#91;that&#93; day &#91;shall be&#93;, The LORD-Yehōvah Shamah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate &#123;is&#125; There&#93;.</span>
</div>
</div>
